// apis/predict/models/actor_critic.mjs
// Advantage Actor-Critic (A2C) with Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE)
//
// Теоретическая основа:
//   - Mnih, V., et al. (2016). "Asynchronous Methods for Deep Reinforcement
//     Learning". ICML 2016. — A3C/A2C.
//   - Schulman, J., Moritz, P., Levine, S., Jordan, M., & Abbeel, P. (2016).
//     "High-Dimensional Continuous Control Using Generalized Advantage
//     Estimation". ICLR 2016. — GAE(γ,λ).
//   - Sutton, R. S., & Barto, A. G. (2018). "Reinforcement Learning:
//     An Introduction" (2nd ed.). MIT Press.
//
// Отличие от DQN (в reinforcement.mjs):
//   DQN — off-policy, обучается на replay buffer, дискретные действия.
//   A2C — on-policy, обучается на свежих траекториях, работает с
//         непрерывными и дискретными действиями, стохастическая политика.
//
// Применение в Crucix:
//   1. AlertPolicyEnv — когда отправлять алерты (silent/normal/critical)
//   2. SourceSelectionEnv — какие источники опрашивать в данном sweep
//   3. ThresholdEnv — оптимизация порогов срабатывания сигналов
//
// Версия: 6.0.0

import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// УТИЛИТЫ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function ensureDir(dir) {
  if (!existsSync(dir)) mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
}

function saveJSON(fp, data) {
  ensureDir(dirname(fp));
  writeFileSync(fp, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

function loadJSON(fp, fallback = null) {
  try {
    return existsSync(fp) ? JSON.parse(readFileSync(fp, 'utf-8')) : fallback;
  } catch { return fallback; }
}

function mean(arr) {
  if (arr.length === 0) return 0;
  return arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length;
}

function std(arr, ddof = 1) {
  const n = arr.length;
  if (n <= ddof) return 0;
  const m = mean(arr);
  return Math.sqrt(arr.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - m) ** 2, 0) / (n - ddof));
}

function dot(a, b) {
  let s = 0;
  const n = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += a[i] * b[i];
  return s;
}

function xavierInit(rows, cols, seed = 42) {
  const scale = Math.sqrt(6 / (rows + cols));
  const W = [];
  let s = seed;
  const rand = () => {
    s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
    return s / 233280;
  };
  for (let i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
    const row = new Float64Array(cols);
    for (let j = 0; j < cols; j++) {
      row[j] = (rand() * 2 - 1) * scale;
    }
    W.push(row);
  }
  return W;
}

function zerosMatrix(rows, cols) {
  return Array.from({ length: rows }, () => new Float64Array(cols));
}

function zerosVec(n) {
  return new Float64Array(n);
}

function relu(x) {
  return Math.max(0, x);
}

function reluDeriv(x) {
  return x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
}

function tanh(x) {
  return Math.tanh(x);
}

function tanhDeriv(x) {
  const t = Math.tanh(x);
  return 1 - t * t;
}

function sigmoid(x) {
  return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-Math.max(-30, Math.min(30, x))));
}

function softmax(arr) {
  const m = Math.max(...arr);
  const e = arr.map(v => Math.exp(v - m));
  const s = e.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  return e.map(v => v / s);
}

function logSoftmax(arr) {
  const m = Math.max(...arr);
  const lse = m + Math.log(arr.reduce((s, v) => s + Math.exp(v - m), 0));
  return arr.map(v => v - lse);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MLP CORE — с полным backprop
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Многослойный перцептрон с ReLU и поддержкой Adam.
 * Используется как backbone и как отдельные heads для A2C.
 */
class MLP {
  constructor({ layers, activation = 'relu', seed = 42 }) {
    this.layers = layers;
    this.activation = activation;
    this.seed = seed;

    this.W = [];
    this.b = [];

    let s = seed;
    for (let i = 0; i < layers.length - 1; i++) {
      const fanIn = layers[i];
      const fanOut = layers[i + 1];
      this.W.push(xavierInit(fanOut, fanIn, s++));
      this.b.push(zerosVec(fanOut));
    }

    // Adam state
    this.mW = this.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)));
    this.vW = this.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)));
    this.mb = this.b.map(bl => zerosVec(bl.length));
    this.vb = this.b.map(bl => zerosVec(bl.length));
    this.t = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Forward pass. Возвращает все активации и пре-активации для backprop.
   */
  forward(x) {
    const activations = [new Float64Array(x)];
    const preActivations = [];

    let current = new Float64Array(x);

    for (let i = 0; i < this.W.length; i++) {
      const z = new Float64Array(this.b[i].length);
      for (let o = 0; o < this.W[i].length; o++) {
        let sum = this.b[i][o];
        for (let j = 0; j < this.W[i][o].length; j++) {
          sum += this.W[i][o][j] * current[j];
        }
        z[o] = sum;
      }
      preActivations.push(z);

      // Activation
      const a = new Float64Array(z.length);
      if (i === this.W.length - 1) {
        // Last layer — линейная (для logits)
        for (let o = 0; o < z.length; o++) a[o] = z[o];
      } else {
        for (let o = 0; o < z.length; o++) {
          if (this.activation === 'relu') a[o] = relu(z[o]);
          else if (this.activation === 'tanh') a[o] = tanh(z[o]);
          else a[o] = z[o];
        }
      }

      activations.push(a);
      current = a;
    }

    return { output: current, activations, preActivations };
  }

  /**
   * Backward pass — градиенты по W и b.
   */
  backward(dOutput, activations, preActivations) {
    const nLayers = this.W.length;
    const dW = this.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)));
    const db = this.b.map(bl => new Float64Array(bl.length));

    let dCurrent = new Float64Array(dOutput);

    for (let l = nLayers - 1; l >= 0; l--) {
      // dZ через производную активации (кроме последнего слоя)
      let dZ;
      if (l === nLayers - 1) {
        dZ = dCurrent;
      } else {
        dZ = new Float64Array(dCurrent.length);
        for (let o = 0; o < dCurrent.length; o++) {
          if (this.activation === 'relu') dZ[o] = dCurrent[o] * reluDeriv(preActivations[l][o]);
          else if (this.activation === 'tanh') dZ[o] = dCurrent[o] * tanhDeriv(preActivations[l][o]);
          else dZ[o] = dCurrent[o];
        }
      }

      // dW = dZ · a_prev^T
      const aPrev = activations[l];
      for (let o = 0; o < this.W[l].length; o++) {
        for (let j = 0; j < this.W[l][o].length; j++) {
          dW[l][o][j] = dZ[o] * aPrev[j];
        }
        db[l][o] = dZ[o];
      }

      // dInput = W^T · dZ
      if (l > 0) {
        const dInput = new Float64Array(aPrev.length);
        for (let j = 0; j < aPrev.length; j++) {
          let sum = 0;
          for (let o = 0; o < this.W[l].length; o++) {
            sum += this.W[l][o][j] * dZ[o];
          }
          dInput[j] = sum;
        }
        dCurrent = dInput;
      }
    }

    return { dW, db };
  }

  /**
   * Adam update.
   */
  update(grads, lr = 0.001) {
    this.t++;
    const beta1 = 0.9, beta2 = 0.999, eps = 1e-8;
    const bc1 = 1 - Math.pow(beta1, this.t);
    const bc2 = 1 - Math.pow(beta2, this.t);

    for (let l = 0; l < this.W.length; l++) {
      for (let o = 0; o < this.W[l].length; o++) {
        for (let j = 0; j < this.W[l][o].length; j++) {
          const g = grads.dW[l][o][j];
          this.mW[l][o][j] = beta1 * this.mW[l][o][j] + (1 - beta1) * g;
          this.vW[l][o][j] = beta2 * this.vW[l][o][j] + (1 - beta2) * g * g;
          const mHat = this.mW[l][o][j] / bc1;
          const vHat = this.vW[l][o][j] / bc2;
          this.W[l][o][j] -= lr * mHat / (Math.sqrt(vHat) + eps);
        }
        const gb = grads.db[l][o];
        this.mb[l][o] = beta1 * this.mb[l][o] + (1 - beta1) * gb;
        this.vb[l][o] = beta2 * this.vb[l][o] + (1 - beta2) * gb * gb;
        const mHat = this.mb[l][o] / bc1;
        const vHat = this.vb[l][o] / bc2;
        this.b[l][o] -= lr * mHat / (Math.sqrt(vHat) + eps);
      }
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ACTOR-CRITIC NETWORK
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Единая сеть A2C: общий backbone + две головы (actor, critic).
 *
 *                ┌─→ Actor Head → π(a|s) — softmax
 *   s → Backbone ┤
 *                └─→ Critic Head → V(s) — linear
 */
class ActorCriticNetwork {
  constructor({ stateDim, actionDim, hiddenDim = 64, nHiddenLayers = 2, seed = 42 }) {
    this.stateDim = stateDim;
    this.actionDim = actionDim;

    // Backbone: MLP [stateDim → hidden × n → hidden]
    const backboneLayers = [stateDim];
    for (let i = 0; i < nHiddenLayers; i++) backboneLayers.push(hiddenDim);
    this.backbone = new MLP({ layers: backboneLayers, activation: 'relu', seed });

    // Actor head: [hidden → actionDim]
    this.actorHead = new MLP({ layers: [hiddenDim, actionDim], activation: 'linear', seed: seed + 100 });

    // Critic head: [hidden → 1]
    this.criticHead = new MLP({ layers: [hiddenDim, 1], activation: 'linear', seed: seed + 200 });
  }

  /**
   * Forward pass: возвращает π(a|s) и V(s).
   */
  forward(state) {
    const backboneResult = this.backbone.forward(state);
    const features = backboneResult.output;

    const actorResult = this.actorHead.forward(features);
    const logits = actorResult.output;
    const probs = softmax(Array.from(logits));

    const criticResult = this.criticHead.forward(features);
    const value = criticResult.output[0];

    return {
      probs,
      logits: Array.from(logits),
      value,
      cache: {
        state,
        backbone: backboneResult,
        features,
        actor: actorResult,
        critic: criticResult,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Sample action из политики.
   */
  sampleAction(state) {
    const { probs, value, cache } = this.forward(state);
    const r = Math.random();
    let cum = 0;
    let action = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < probs.length; i++) {
      cum += probs[i];
      if (r < cum) { action = i; break; }
    }
    return { action, prob: probs[action], probs, value, cache };
  }

  /**
   * Полный backward pass:
   *   L = policy_loss + value_coef · value_loss - entropy_coef · entropy
   *
   * policy_loss = -log π(a|s) · A(s,a)
   * value_loss = (V(s) - target)²
   * entropy = -Σ π(a|s) · log π(a|s)
   */
  backward(action, advantage, valueTarget, cache, config = {}) {
    const { valueCoef = 0.5, entropyCoef = 0.01 } = config;
    const { probs, value, cache: innerCache } = cache;
    const logProbs = logSoftmax(innerCache.actor.output);

    // ─── Actor gradients ───
    // dL_actor/dlogits = probs - one_hot(action)
    // умножаем на advantage (с минусом, т.к. loss = -advantage · logπ)
    const dLogits = new Float64Array(this.actionDim);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.actionDim; i++) {
      dLogits[i] = probs[i] - (i === action ? 1 : 0);
    }
    // Градиент с учётом advantage
    for (let i = 0; i < this.actionDim; i++) {
      dLogits[i] *= -advantage;
    }

    // Entropy gradient: d(-H)/dlogits = -probs · (log probs + H)
    // H = -Σ p·log p
    let H = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.actionDim; i++) {
      H -= probs[i] * logProbs[i];
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < this.actionDim; i++) {
      // d(-entropyCoef · H)/dlogits[i] = entropyCoef · probs[i] · (logProbs[i] + H)
      dLogits[i] += entropyCoef * probs[i] * (logProbs[i] + H);
    }

    const actorGrads = this.actorHead.backward(
      dLogits,
      innerCache.actor.activations,
      innerCache.actor.preActivations
    );

    // ─── Critic gradients ───
    // dL_critic/dValue = 2 · valueCoef · (V - target)
    const dValue = new Float64Array([2 * valueCoef * (value - valueTarget)]);
    const criticGrads = this.criticHead.backward(
      dValue,
      innerCache.critic.activations,
      innerCache.critic.preActivations
    );

    // ─── Backbone gradients ───
    // Собираем dFeatures из обеих голов
    const dFeatures = new Float64Array(innerCache.features.length);
    const actorDInput = this._getInputGrad(actorGrads.dW, innerCache.actor.activations, 0);
    // Упрощённо — используем dConcat через прямой backward
    // В actorHead последний слой → features, dW[0] уже посчитан
    // Правильный путь: backward на MLP возвращает градиент по входу.
    // Здесь мы вызываем ещё раз для получения dInput.
    const actorBackward = this._computeInputGrad(this.actorHead, dLogits, innerCache.actor);
    const criticBackward = this._computeInputGrad(this.criticHead, dValue, innerCache.critic);

    for (let i = 0; i < dFeatures.length; i++) {
      dFeatures[i] = actorBackward[i] + criticBackward[i];
    }

    const backboneGrads = this.backbone.backward(
      dFeatures,
      innerCache.backbone.activations,
      innerCache.backbone.preActivations
    );

    return {
      actor: actorGrads,
      critic: criticGrads,
      backbone: backboneGrads,
      entropy: H,
      valueLoss: (value - valueTarget) ** 2,
    };
  }

  _computeInputGrad(mlp, dOutput, cache) {
    // Прямой проход backward, собираем градиент по входу
    const dInput = new Float64Array(mlp.layers[0]);
    // Начинаем с dOutput
    let dCurrent = dOutput;

    for (let l = mlp.W.length - 1; l >= 0; l--) {
      // Производная активации (кроме последнего слоя)
      let dZ;
      if (l === mlp.W.length - 1) {
        dZ = dCurrent;
      } else {
        dZ = new Float64Array(dCurrent.length);
        for (let o = 0; o < dCurrent.length; o++) {
          if (mlp.activation === 'relu') dZ[o] = dCurrent[o] * reluDeriv(cache.preActivations[l][o]);
          else if (mlp.activation === 'tanh') dZ[o] = dCurrent[o] * tanhDeriv(cache.preActivations[l][o]);
          else dZ[o] = dCurrent[o];
        }
      }

      // dInput = W^T · dZ
      const aPrev = cache.activations[l];
      const dIn = new Float64Array(aPrev.length);
      for (let j = 0; j < aPrev.length; j++) {
        let sum = 0;
        for (let o = 0; o < mlp.W[l].length; o++) {
          sum += mlp.W[l][o][j] * dZ[o];
        }
        dIn[j] = sum;
      }
      dCurrent = dIn;
    }

    return dCurrent;
  }

  /**
   * Обновление весов.
   */
  update(grads, lr = 0.001) {
    this.backbone.update(grads.backbone, lr);
    this.actorHead.update(grads.actor, lr);
    this.criticHead.update(grads.critic, lr);
  }

  serialize() {
    return {
      config: { stateDim: this.stateDim, actionDim: this.actionDim },
      backbone: {
        W: this.backbone.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(r => Array.from(r))),
        b: this.backbone.b.map(bl => Array.from(bl)),
      },
      actor: {
        W: this.actorHead.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(r => Array.from(r))),
        b: this.actorHead.b.map(bl => Array.from(bl)),
      },
      critic: {
        W: this.criticHead.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(r => Array.from(r))),
        b: this.criticHead.b.map(bl => Array.from(bl)),
      },
    };
  }

  static deserialize(data) {
    const net = new ActorCriticNetwork({
      stateDim: data.config.stateDim,
      actionDim: data.config.actionDim,
      hiddenDim: data.backbone.W[0].length, // fan-out
    });
    net.backbone.W = data.backbone.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(r => new Float64Array(r)));
    net.backbone.b = data.backbone.b.map(bl => new Float64Array(bl));
    net.actorHead.W = data.actor.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(r => new Float64Array(r)));
    net.actorHead.b = data.actor.b.map(bl => new Float64Array(bl));
    net.criticHead.W = data.critic.W.map(Wl => Wl.map(r => new Float64Array(r)));
    net.criticHead.b = data.critic.b.map(bl => new Float64Array(bl));
    return net;
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// GAE — Generalized Advantage Estimation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * GAE(γ, λ):
 *   δ_t = r_t + γ·V(s_{t+1}) - V(s_t)
 *   A_t = Σ_{l=0}^{∞} (γλ)^l · δ_{t+l}
 *
 * λ=0 → одношаговый advantage (низкая variance, высокий bias).
 * λ=1 → полный Monte-Carlo return (низкий bias, высокая variance).
 * Обычно λ=0.95.
 *
 * @param {Object[]} trajectory — [{ state, action, reward, value, done }]
 * @param {number} gamma — дисконт (0.99)
 * @param {number} lambda — GAE λ (0.95)
 * @param {number} lastValue — V(s_T) для bootstrap
 * @returns {Object} — { advantages, returns }
 */
function computeGAE(trajectory, gamma = 0.99, lambda = 0.95, lastValue = 0) {
  const T = trajectory.length;
  const advantages = new Float64Array(T);
  const returns = new Float64Array(T);

  let gae = 0;
  for (let t = T - 1; t >= 0; t--) {
    const { reward, value, done } = trajectory[t];
    const nextValue = t === T - 1 ? lastValue : trajectory[t + 1].value;
    const delta = reward + gamma * nextValue * (done ? 0 : 1) - value;
    gae = delta + gamma * lambda * (done ? 0 : 1) * gae;
    advantages[t] = gae;
    returns[t] = gae + value;
  }

  return { advantages, returns };
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// A2C AGENT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Advantage Actor-Critic агент.
 *
 * On-policy: собирает траекторию, обновляется, очищает буфер.
 * Может работать с n-step returns или GAE.
 */
class A2CAgent {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.stateDim = config.stateDim || 10;
    this.actionDim = config.actionDim || 3;
    this.hiddenDim = config.hiddenDim || 64;
    this.nHiddenLayers = config.nHiddenLayers || 2;
    this.learningRate = config.learningRate || 0.0007;
    this.gamma = config.gamma ?? 0.99;
    this.lambda = config.lambda ?? 0.95;
    this.valueCoef = config.valueCoef ?? 0.5;
    this.entropyCoef = config.entropyCoef ?? 0.01;
    this.maxGradNorm = config.maxGradNorm ?? 0.5;
    this.useGAE = config.useGAE !== false;

    this.network = new ActorCriticNetwork({
      stateDim: this.stateDim,
      actionDim: this.actionDim,
      hiddenDim: this.hiddenDim,
      nHiddenLayers: this.nHiddenLayers,
    });

    this.trajectory = [];
    this.stats = {
      episodes: 0,
      steps: 0,
      avgReward: 0,
      avgEntropy: 0,
      avgValueLoss: 0,
    };
    this.history = [];
  }

  /**
   * Выбор действия.
   */
  selectAction(state) {
    const { action, prob, probs, value, cache } = this.network.sampleAction(state);
    return { action, prob, probs, value, cache };
  }

  /**
   * Сохранение шага.
   */
  store(state, action, reward, value, done, cache = null) {
    this.trajectory.push({ state, action, reward, value, done, cache });
    this.stats.steps++;
  }

  /**
   * Обновление сети после эпизода.
   */
  update(lastValue = 0) {
    const T = this.trajectory.length;
    if (T < 2) return { updated: false, reason: 'trajectory_too_short' };

    // GAE
    const { advantages, returns } = computeGAE(
      this.trajectory,
      this.gamma,
      this.lambda,
      lastValue
    );

    // Нормализация advantages
    const meanAdv = mean(Array.from(advantages));
    const stdAdv = std(Array.from(advantages)) || 1;
    const normalizedAdv = Array.from(advantages).map(a => (a - meanAdv) / stdAdv);

    // Обучение
    let totalEntropy = 0;
    let totalValueLoss = 0;
    let totalPolicyLoss = 0;

    for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
      const step = this.trajectory[t];

      // Forward для получения свежего cache
      const forward = this.network.forward(step.state);
      const cache = {
        probs: forward.probs,
        value: forward.value,
        cache: forward.cache,
      };

      const grads = this.network.backward(
        step.action,
        normalizedAdv[t],
        returns[t],
        cache,
        { valueCoef: this.valueCoef, entropyCoef: this.entropyCoef }
      );

      // Gradient clipping
      const gradsClipped = this._clipGrads(grads, this.maxGradNorm);

      // Update
      this.network.update(gradsClipped, this.learningRate);

      totalEntropy += grads.entropy;
      totalValueLoss += grads.valueLoss;
      // Policy loss: -log π(a|s) · A(s,a)
      const logProb = Math.log(Math.max(cache.probs[step.action], 1e-10));
      totalPolicyLoss += -logProb * normalizedAdv[t];
    }

    const avgReward = mean(this.trajectory.map(t => t.reward));

    this.stats.episodes++;
    this.stats.avgReward = (this.stats.avgReward * (this.stats.episodes - 1) + avgReward) / this.stats.episodes;
    this.stats.avgEntropy = totalEntropy / T;
    this.stats.avgValueLoss = totalValueLoss / T;

    this.history.push({
      episode: this.stats.episodes,
      avgReward,
      policyLoss: totalPolicyLoss / T,
      valueLoss: totalValueLoss / T,
      entropy: totalEntropy / T,
      steps: T,
    });

    // Очистка
    this.trajectory = [];

    return {
      updated: true,
      avgReward,
      policyLoss: totalPolicyLoss / T,
      valueLoss: totalValueLoss / T,
      entropy: totalEntropy / T,
      steps: T,
    };
  }

  _clipGrads(grads, maxNorm) {
    // Собираем все градиенты, считаем норму
    let totalNormSq = 0;
    const collect = (mats) => {
      for (const M of mats) {
        for (const row of M) {
          for (const v of row) {
            totalNormSq += v * v;
          }
        }
      }
    };
    const collectVecs = (vecs) => {
      for (const v of vecs) {
        for (const x of v) totalNormSq += x * x;
      }
    };

    collect(grads.backbone.dW);
    collect(grads.actor.dW);
    collect(grads.critic.dW);
    collectVecs(grads.backbone.db);
    collectVecs(grads.actor.db);
    collectVecs(grads.critic.db);

    const norm = Math.sqrt(totalNormSq);
    if (norm <= maxNorm) return grads;

    const scale = maxNorm / norm;
    const scaleMat = (mats) => mats.map(M => M.map(row => row.map(v => v * scale)));
    const scaleVecs = (vecs) => vecs.map(v => v.map(x => x * scale));

    return {
      backbone: {
        dW: scaleMat(grads.backbone.dW),
        db: scaleVecs(grads.backbone.db),
      },
      actor: {
        dW: scaleMat(grads.actor.dW),
        db: scaleVecs(grads.actor.db),
      },
      critic: {
        dW: scaleMat(grads.critic.dW),
        db: scaleVecs(grads.critic.db),
      },
      entropy: grads.entropy,
      valueLoss: grads.valueLoss,
    };
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// СРЕДЫ ДЛЯ CRUCIX
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * AlertPolicyEnv: решает когда отправлять алерты.
 *
 * Действия:
 *   0 — silent (не отправлять)
 *   1 — normal_alert
 *   2 — critical_alert
 *
 * State (10 признаков):
 *   [vix/50, hySpread/10, conflicts/20, sanctions/10, newAlerts/10,
 *    escalated/5, radiation/200, oil/120, gold/2200, dxy/110]
 *
 * Reward:
 *   - Правильный алерт на серьёзное событие: +1..+2
 *   - Ложный алерт: -0.4..-0.6
 *   - Молчание когда надо было: -0.8
 *   - Молчание когда не надо было: +0.2
 */
class AlertPolicyEnv {
  constructor(history) {
    this.history = history;
    this.currentStep = 0;
    this.maxSteps = history.length - 2;
  }

  reset() {
    this.currentStep = 0;
    return this._getState();
  }

  _getState() {
    const h = this.history[this.currentStep];
    if (!h) return new Float64Array(10);

    return new Float64Array([
      (h.fred?.vix ?? 20) / 50,
      (h.fred?.hySpread ?? 3) / 10,
      (h.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length ?? 0) / 20,
      (h.sanctions?.count ?? 0) / 10,
      (h.delta?.newAlerts ?? 0) / 10,
      (h.delta?.escalatedAlerts ?? 0) / 5,
      (h.radiation ? Math.max(...Object.values(h.radiation).map(r => r.cpm || 0)) : 0) / 200,
      (h.energy?.oilPrice ?? 70) / 120,
      (h.gold?.price ?? 1900) / 2200,
      (h.dxy?.value ?? 100) / 110,
    ]);
  }

  step(action) {
    const next = this.history[this.currentStep + 1];
    const future = this.history[this.currentStep + 2];

    if (!next) {
      return {
        nextState: this._getState(),
        reward: 0,
        done: true,
      };
    }

    const nextVix = next.fred?.vix ?? 20;
    const futureVix = future?.fred?.vix ?? nextVix;
    const nextConflicts = next.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length ?? 0;

    const isSerious = nextVix > 28 || nextConflicts > 12 || futureVix > 32;

    let reward;
    if (action === 0) {
      reward = isSerious ? -0.8 : 0.2;
    } else if (action === 1) {
      reward = isSerious ? 1.0 : -0.4;
    } else {
      reward = (isSerious && nextVix > 30) ? 2.0 : -0.6;
    }

    this.currentStep++;
    const done = this.currentStep >= this.maxSteps;

    return {
      nextState: this._getState(),
      reward,
      done,
      info: { isSerious, nextVix, nextConflicts },
    };
  }
}

/**
 * SourceSelectionEnv: какие источники опрашивать.
 *
 * Действия:
 *   0 — normal sweep (только базовые)
 *   1 — add extra sources (medium)
 *   2 — deep scan (все)
 *
 * State — те же 10 признаков.
 * Reward — за точность прогноза при заданном уровне сканирования.
 */
class SourceSelectionEnv {
  constructor(history) {
    this.history = history;
    this.currentStep = 0;
    this.maxSteps = history.length - 2;
  }

  reset() {
    this.currentStep = 0;
    return this._getState();
  }

  _getState() {
    const h = this.history[this.currentStep];
    if (!h) return new Float64Array(10);
    return new Float64Array([
      (h.fred?.vix ?? 20) / 50,
      (h.fred?.hySpread ?? 3) / 10,
      (h.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length ?? 0) / 20,
      (h.sanctions?.count ?? 0) / 10,
      (h.delta?.newAlerts ?? 0) / 10,
      (h.delta?.escalatedAlerts ?? 0) / 5,
      (h.radiation ? Math.max(...Object.values(h.radiation).map(r => r.cpm || 0)) : 0) / 200,
      (h.energy?.oilPrice ?? 70) / 120,
      (h.gold?.price ?? 1900) / 2200,
      (h.dxy?.value ?? 100) / 110,
    ]);
  }

  step(action) {
    const next = this.history[this.currentStep + 1];
    if (!next) {
      return { nextState: this._getState(), reward: 0, done: true };
    }

    const vix = next.fred?.vix ?? 20;
    const conflicts = next.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length ?? 0;
    const isHighRisk = vix > 28 || conflicts > 12;

    // Reward: правильное использование ресурсов
    let reward;
    if (isHighRisk) {
      // В высоком риске нужно больше данных
      reward = action === 2 ? 1.0 : action === 1 ? 0.6 : -0.5;
    } else {
      // В спокойствии не тратим ресурсы
      reward = action === 0 ? 0.5 : action === 1 ? -0.1 : -0.4;
    }

    this.currentStep++;
    return {
      nextState: this._getState(),
      reward,
      done: this.currentStep >= this.maxSteps,
    };
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ОБУЧЕНИЕ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Обучение A2C агента на среде.
 */
function trainA2C(env, { episodes = 100, stateDim = 10, actionDim = 3, verbose = false } = {}) {
  const agent = new A2CAgent({
    stateDim,
    actionDim,
    hiddenDim: 64,
    learningRate: 0.0007,
    gamma: 0.99,
    lambda: 0.95,
    valueCoef: 0.5,
    entropyCoef: 0.01,
  });

  const history = [];

  for (let ep = 0; ep < episodes; ep++) {
    let state = env.reset();
    let done = false;
    let episodeReward = 0;

    while (!done) {
      const { action, value } = agent.selectAction(state);
      const { nextState, reward, done: isDone } = env.step(action);

      agent.store(state, action, reward, value, isDone);
      episodeReward += reward;
      state = nextState;
      done = isDone;
    }

    // Bootstrap value для последнего состояния
    const lastState = state;
    const { value: lastValue } = agent.network.forward(lastState);

    const updateResult = agent.update(lastValue);

    history.push({
      episode: ep,
      reward: episodeReward,
      ...updateResult,
    });

    if (verbose && ep % 10 === 0) {
      console.log(`[a2c] episode ${ep}: reward=${episodeReward.toFixed(2)}, policyLoss=${updateResult.policyLoss?.toFixed(4)}, entropy=${updateResult.entropy?.toFixed(4)}`);
    }
  }

  return { agent, history };
}

/**
 * Оценка обученной политики.
 */
function evaluateA2C(agent, env, nEpisodes = 20) {
  let totalReward = 0;
  const actionCounts = new Array(agent.actionDim).fill(0);

  for (let ep = 0; ep < nEpisodes; ep++) {
    let state = env.reset();
    let done = false;

    while (!done) {
      const { probs } = agent.network.forward(state);
      const action = probs.indexOf(Math.max(...probs)); // greedy
      actionCounts[action]++;
      const { nextState, reward, done: isDone } = env.step(action);
      totalReward += reward;
      state = nextState;
      done = isDone;
    }
  }

  const actionDistribution = actionCounts.map(c => c / actionCounts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0));

  return {
    avgReward: totalReward / nEpisodes,
    actionDistribution,
    nEpisodes,
  };
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ГЛАВНАЯ ФУНКЦИЯ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Полный A2C-цикл для Crucix.
 *
 * @param {Array} history — массив sweep-объектов
 * @param {Object} options — параметры
 * @returns {Object} — результат обучения и политика
 */
export function crucixActorCritic(history, options = {}) {
  if (!history || history.length < 30) {
    return {
      module: 'actor_critic',
      available: false,
      reason: 'insufficient_history',
      minimumRequired: 30,
      actual: history.length,
    };
  }

  const t0 = Date.now();
  console.log(`[actor_critic] Запуск на ${history.length} sweep'ах`);

  const result = {
    module: 'actor_critic',
    available: true,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    nSweeps: history.length,
  };

  // ─── Alert Policy ───
  try {
    const env = new AlertPolicyEnv(history);
    const { agent, history: trainHistory } = trainA2C(env, {
      episodes: options.episodes || 50,
      stateDim: 10,
      actionDim: 3,
      verbose: false,
    });

    const evalResult = evaluateA2C(agent, env, 20);
    const actionNames = ['silent', 'normal_alert', 'critical_alert'];

    result.alertPolicy = {
      avgReward: Math.round(evalResult.avgReward * 1000) / 1000,
      actionDistribution: {
        silent: Math.round(evalResult.actionDistribution[0] * 1000) / 1000,
        normal_alert: Math.round(evalResult.actionDistribution[1] * 1000) / 1000,
        critical_alert: Math.round(evalResult.actionDistribution[2] * 1000) / 1000,
      },
      trainingEpisodes: trainHistory.length,
      finalReward: Math.round((trainHistory[trainHistory.length - 1]?.reward ?? 0) * 100) / 100,
      firstReward: Math.round((trainHistory[0]?.reward ?? 0) * 100) / 100,
      learningProgress: trainHistory.length > 0
        ? Math.round((trainHistory[trainHistory.length - 1]?.reward ?? 0) -
                     (trainHistory[0]?.reward ?? 0)) * 100 / 100
        : 0,
      finalEntropy: Math.round((trainHistory[trainHistory.length - 1]?.entropy ?? 0) * 1000) / 1000,
    };

    // Сохранение модели
    const dir = join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'runs', 'predictions');
    ensureDir(dir);
    saveJSON(join(dir, 'actor_critic_alert.json'), agent.network.serialize());
  } catch (e) {
    result.alertPolicy = { error: e.message };
  }

  // ─── Source Selection ───
  try {
    const env = new SourceSelectionEnv(history);
    const { agent, history: trainHistory } = trainA2C(env, {
      episodes: options.episodes || 50,
      stateDim: 10,
      actionDim: 3,
      verbose: false,
    });

    const evalResult = evaluateA2C(agent, env, 20);

    result.sourceSelection = {
      avgReward: Math.round(evalResult.avgReward * 1000) / 1000,
      actionDistribution: {
        normal_sweep: Math.round(evalResult.actionDistribution[0] * 1000) / 1000,
        add_sources: Math.round(evalResult.actionDistribution[1] * 1000) / 1000,
        deep_scan: Math.round(evalResult.actionDistribution[2] * 1000) / 1000,
      },
      finalReward: Math.round((trainHistory[trainHistory.length - 1]?.reward ?? 0) * 100) / 100,
      firstReward: Math.round((trainHistory[0]?.reward ?? 0) * 100) / 100,
    };

    const dir = join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'runs', 'predictions');
    ensureDir(dir);
    saveJSON(join(dir, 'actor_critic_source.json'), agent.network.serialize());
  } catch (e) {
    result.sourceSelection = { error: e.message };
  }

  result.interpretation = result.alertPolicy?.avgReward > 0
    ? `A2C обучен. Alert policy avg reward: ${result.alertPolicy.avgReward}`
    : 'A2C не смог обучиться';

  result.elapsedMs = Date.now() - t0;

  // Сохранение общего результата
  const dir = join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'runs', 'predictions');
  ensureDir(dir);
  saveJSON(join(dir, 'actor_critic.json'), result);

  console.log(`[actor_critic] Цикл завершён за ${result.elapsedMs}ms`);
  return result;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ЭКСПОРТ

export {
  A2CAgent,
  ActorCriticNetwork,
  MLP,
  AlertPolicyEnv,
  SourceSelectionEnv,
  computeGAE,
  trainA2C,
  evaluateA2C,
};

// apis/predict/models/transformer.mjs
// Transformer с полным backprop (Vaswani et al., 2017)
import { softmax as wasmSoftmax } from '../wasm/simd_loader.mjs';

function xavier(rows, cols, seed = 42) { const scale = Math.sqrt(2 / (rows + cols)); const W = []; let s = seed; const rand = () => { s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; return s / 233280; }; for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) { const col = new Float64Array(rows); for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { const u1 = rand(), u2 = rand(); const z = Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1 + 1e-10)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2); col[j] = z * scale; } W.push(col); } return W; }
function matmul(A, B) { const n = A.length, m = A[0].length, p = B[0].length; const C = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(p)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let k = 0; k < m; k++) { const aik = A[i][k]; if (aik === 0) continue; for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) C[i][j] += aik * B[k][j]; } return C; }
function transpose(A) { const n = A.length, m = A[0].length; const T = Array.from({ length: m }, () => new Float64Array(n)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) T[j][i] = A[i][j]; return T; }
function positionalEncoding(seqLen, dModel) { const PE = Array.from({ length: seqLen }, () => new Float64Array(dModel)); for (let pos = 0; pos < seqLen; pos++) for (let i = 0; i < dModel; i += 2) { const div = Math.pow(10000, i / dModel); PE[pos][i] = Math.sin(pos / div); if (i + 1 < dModel) PE[pos][i + 1] = Math.cos(pos / div); } return PE; }
function softmaxWasm(x) { const max = Math.max(...x); const exps = x.map(v => Math.exp(v - max)); const sum = exps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); return exps.map(v => v / sum); }

class LayerNorm {
  constructor(dModel, eps = 1e-6) { this.dModel = dModel; this.eps = eps; this.gamma = new Float64Array(dModel).fill(1); this.beta = new Float64Array(dModel).fill(0); }
  forward(X) { const n = X.length; const Y = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dModel)); const cache = []; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = X[i]; let mean = 0; for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) mean += x[j]; mean /= this.dModel; let variance = 0; for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) variance += (x[j] - mean) ** 2; variance /= this.dModel; const std = Math.sqrt(variance + this.eps); for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { const xhat = (x[j] - mean) / std; Y[i][j] = this.gamma[j] * xhat + this.beta[j]; } cache.push({ mean, std, xhat: Array.from(x).map(v => (v - mean) / std) }); } return { output: Y, cache }; }
  backward(dY, X, cache) { const n = X.length; const dX = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dModel)); const dGamma = new Float64Array(this.dModel); const dBeta = new Float64Array(this.dModel); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const { std, xhat } = cache[i]; let dXhatSum = 0, dXhatXhatSum = 0; for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { const dXhat = dY[i][j] * this.gamma[j]; dXhatSum += dXhat; dXhatXhatSum += dXhat * xhat[j]; dGamma[j] += dY[i][j] * xhat[j]; dBeta[j] += dY[i][j]; } for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { const dXhat = dY[i][j] * this.gamma[j]; dX[i][j] = (1 / std) * (dXhat - dXhatSum / this.dModel - xhat[j] * dXhatXhatSum / this.dModel); } } return { dX, dGamma, dBeta }; }
  updateGradients(dGamma, dBeta, lr = 0.01) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { this.gamma[j] -= lr * dGamma[j]; this.beta[j] -= lr * dBeta[j]; } }
}

class MultiHeadAttention {
  constructor(dModel, nHeads, seed = 42) { if (dModel % nHeads !== 0) throw new Error('dModel должен делиться на nHeads'); this.dModel = dModel; this.nHeads = nHeads; this.dHead = dModel / nHeads; this.Wq = xavier(dModel, dModel, seed); this.Wk = xavier(dModel, dModel, seed + 1); this.Wv = xavier(dModel, dModel, seed + 2); this.Wo = xavier(dModel, dModel, seed + 3); this.bq = new Float64Array(dModel); this.bk = new Float64Array(dModel); this.bv = new Float64Array(dModel); this.bo = new Float64Array(dModel); }
  forward(X, mask = null) { const n = X.length; const d = this.dModel; const h = this.nHeads; const dh = this.dHead; const Q = this._project(X, this.Wq, this.bq); const K = this._project(X, this.Wk, this.bk); const V = this._project(X, this.Wv, this.bv); const Qh = this._splitHeads(Q); const Kh = this._splitHeads(K); const Vh = this._splitHeads(V); const attentionScores = []; const attentionWeights = []; const headOutputs = []; for (let hIdx = 0; hIdx < h; hIdx++) { const scores = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(n)); const scale = 1 / Math.sqrt(dh); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) { let dot = 0; for (let k = 0; k < dh; k++) dot += Qh[hIdx][i][k] * Kh[hIdx][j][k]; scores[i][j] = dot * scale; if (mask && mask[i][j] === 0) scores[i][j] = -1e9; } const weights = scores.map(row => softmaxWasm(Array.from(row))); attentionScores.push(scores); attentionWeights.push(weights); const headOut = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(dh)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const w = weights[i][k]; if (w === 0) continue; for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) headOut[i][j] += w * Vh[hIdx][k][j]; } headOutputs.push(headOut); } const concat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let hIdx = 0; hIdx < h; hIdx++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) concat[i][hIdx * dh + j] = headOutputs[hIdx][i][j]; const output = this._project(concat, this.Wo, this.bo); const cache = { Q, K, V, Qh, Kh, Vh, attentionWeights, headOutputs, concat, X, n, mask }; return { output, attention: attentionWeights, cache }; }
  _project(X, W, b) { const n = X.length; const d = W.length; const Y = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let o = 0; o < d; o++) { let sum = b[o]; for (let j = 0; j < X[i].length; j++) sum += X[i][j] * W[o][j]; Y[i][o] = sum; } return Y; }
  _splitHeads(X) { const n = X.length; const d = this.dModel; const h = this.nHeads; const dh = this.dHead; const result = []; for (let hIdx = 0; hIdx < h; hIdx++) { const head = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(dh)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) head[i][j] = X[i][hIdx * dh + j]; result.push(head); } return result; }
  backward(dOutput, cache) { const { Q, K, V, Qh, Kh, Vh, attentionWeights, headOutputs, concat, X, n } = cache; const d = this.dModel; const h = this.nHeads; const dh = this.dHead; const dWq = this.Wq.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const dWk = this.Wk.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const dWv = this.Wv.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const dWo = this.Wo.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const dbq = new Float64Array(d); const dbk = new Float64Array(d); const dbv = new Float64Array(d); const dbo = new Float64Array(d); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let o = 0; o < d; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) dWo[o][j] += dOutput[i][o] * concat[i][j]; dbo[o] += dOutput[i][o]; } const dConcat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < d; o++) sum += dOutput[i][o] * this.Wo[o][j]; dConcat[i][j] = sum; } const dHeadOutputs = []; for (let hIdx = 0; hIdx < h; hIdx++) { const head = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(dh)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) head[i][j] = dConcat[i][hIdx * dh + j]; dHeadOutputs.push(head); } const dQh = Array.from({ length: h }, () => Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(dh))); const dKh = Array.from({ length: h }, () => Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(dh))); const dVh = Array.from({ length: h }, () => Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(dh))); const scale = 1 / Math.sqrt(dh); for (let hIdx = 0; hIdx < h; hIdx++) { for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sum += attentionWeights[hIdx][i][k] * dHeadOutputs[hIdx][i][j]; dVh[hIdx][k][j] = sum; } const dWeights = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(n)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { let sum = 0; for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) sum += dHeadOutputs[hIdx][i][j] * Vh[hIdx][k][j]; dWeights[i][k] = sum; } const dScores = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(n)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const w = attentionWeights[hIdx][i]; const dw = dWeights[i]; let dotProduct = 0; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) dotProduct += w[k] * dw[k]; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) dScores[i][k] = w[k] * (dw[k] - dotProduct); } for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) sum += dScores[i][k] * Kh[hIdx][k][j]; dQh[hIdx][i][j] = sum * scale; } for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sum += dScores[i][k] * Qh[hIdx][i][j]; dKh[hIdx][k][j] = sum * scale; } } const dQ = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); const dK = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); const dV = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let hIdx = 0; hIdx < h; hIdx++) for (let j = 0; j < dh; j++) { dQ[i][hIdx * dh + j] = dQh[hIdx][i][j]; dK[i][hIdx * dh + j] = dKh[hIdx][i][j]; dV[i][hIdx * dh + j] = dVh[hIdx][i][j]; } for (let o = 0; o < d; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) { let sumQ = 0, sumK = 0, sumV = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { sumQ += dQ[i][o] * X[i][j]; sumK += dK[i][o] * X[i][j]; sumV += dV[i][o] * X[i][j]; } dWq[o][j] = sumQ; dWk[o][j] = sumK; dWv[o][j] = sumV; } let bqSum = 0, bkSum = 0, bvSum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { bqSum += dQ[i][o]; bkSum += dK[i][o]; bvSum += dV[i][o]; } dbq[o] = bqSum; dbk[o] = bkSum; dbv[o] = bvSum; } const dX = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(d)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < d; o++) sum += dQ[i][o] * this.Wq[o][j] + dK[i][o] * this.Wk[o][j] + dV[i][o] * this.Wv[o][j]; dX[i][j] = sum; } return { dX, dWq, dWk, dWv, dWo, dbq, dbk, dbv, dbo }; }
  updateGradients(grads, lr = 0.001) { const { dWq, dWk, dWv, dWo, dbq, dbk, dbv, dbo } = grads; for (let o = 0; o < this.dModel; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { this.Wq[o][j] -= lr * dWq[o][j]; this.Wk[o][j] -= lr * dWk[o][j]; this.Wv[o][j] -= lr * dWv[o][j]; this.Wo[o][j] -= lr * dWo[o][j]; } this.bq[o] -= lr * dbq[o]; this.bk[o] -= lr * dbk[o]; this.bv[o] -= lr * dbv[o]; this.bo[o] -= lr * dbo[o]; } }
}

class FeedForward {
  constructor(dModel, dFF, seed = 42) { this.dModel = dModel; this.dFF = dFF; this.W1 = xavier(dModel, dFF, seed); this.b1 = new Float64Array(dFF); this.W2 = xavier(dFF, dModel, seed + 1); this.b2 = new Float64Array(dModel); }
  forward(X) { const n = X.length; const H = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dFF)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let o = 0; o < this.dFF; o++) { let sum = this.b1[o]; for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) sum += X[i][j] * this.W1[o][j]; H[i][o] = Math.max(0, sum); } const Y = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dModel)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let o = 0; o < this.dModel; o++) { let sum = this.b2[o]; for (let j = 0; j < this.dFF; j++) sum += H[i][j] * this.W2[o][j]; Y[i][o] = sum; } return { output: Y, cache: { X, H } }; }
  backward(dY, cache) { const { X, H } = cache; const n = X.length; const dW1 = this.W1.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const dW2 = this.W2.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const db1 = new Float64Array(this.dFF); const db2 = new Float64Array(this.dModel); const dH = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dFF)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < this.dFF; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < this.dModel; o++) sum += dY[i][o] * this.W2[o][j]; dH[i][j] = H[i][j] > 0 ? sum : 0; } for (let o = 0; o < this.dModel; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dFF; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sum += H[i][j] * dY[i][o]; dW2[o][j] = sum; } let bSum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) bSum += dY[i][o]; db2[o] = bSum; } for (let o = 0; o < this.dFF; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sum += X[i][j] * dH[i][o]; dW1[o][j] = sum; } let bSum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) bSum += dH[i][o]; db1[o] = bSum; } const dX = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dModel)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < this.dFF; o++) sum += dH[i][o] * this.W1[o][j]; dX[i][j] = sum; } return { dX, dW1, dW2, db1, db2 }; }
  updateGradients(grads, lr = 0.001) { const { dW1, dW2, db1, db2 } = grads; for (let o = 0; o < this.dFF; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) this.W1[o][j] -= lr * dW1[o][j]; this.b1[o] -= lr * db1[o]; } for (let o = 0; o < this.dModel; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dFF; j++) this.W2[o][j] -= lr * dW2[o][j]; this.b2[o] -= lr * db2[o]; } }
}

class TransformerBlock {
  constructor(dModel, nHeads, dFF, seed = 42) { this.attention = new MultiHeadAttention(dModel, nHeads, seed); this.norm1 = new LayerNorm(dModel); this.ff = new FeedForward(dModel, dFF, seed + 10); this.norm2 = new LayerNorm(dModel); }
  forward(X, mask = null) { const { output: attnOut, cache: attnCache } = this.attention.forward(X, mask); const res1 = X.map((row, i) => row.map((v, j) => v + attnOut[i][j])); const { output: norm1Out, cache: norm1Cache } = this.norm1.forward(res1); const { output: ffOut, cache: ffCache } = this.ff.forward(norm1Out); const res2 = norm1Out.map((row, i) => row.map((v, j) => v + ffOut[i][j])); const { output: finalOut, cache: norm2Cache } = this.norm2.forward(res2); return { output: finalOut, cache: { X, attnOut, attnCache, res1, norm1Out, norm1Cache, ffOut, ffCache, res2, norm2Cache } }; }
  backward(dOut, cache) { const { res1, norm1Out, ffCache, res2, norm2Cache } = cache; const { dX: dNorm2 } = this.norm2.backward(dOut, res2, norm2Cache); const dFFOut = dNorm2.map(row => new Float64Array(row)); const ffGrads = this.ff.backward(dFFOut, ffCache); const dNorm1Out = dNorm2.map((row, i) => row.map((v, j) => v + ffGrads.dX[i][j])); const { dX: dRes1 } = this.norm1.backward(dNorm1Out, res1, cache.norm1Cache); const dAttnOut = dRes1.map(row => new Float64Array(row)); const attnGrads = this.attention.backward(dAttnOut, cache.attnCache); const dX = dRes1.map((row, i) => row.map((v, j) => v + attnGrads.dX[i][j])); return { dX, attnGrads, ffGrads, norm1Grads: this.norm1.backward(dNorm1Out, res1, cache.norm1Cache), norm2Grads: this.norm2.backward(dOut, res2, norm2Cache) }; }
  updateGradients(grads, lr = 0.001) { this.attention.updateGradients(grads.attnGrads, lr); this.ff.updateGradients(grads.ffGrads, lr); this.norm1.updateGradients(grads.norm1Grads.dGamma, grads.norm1Grads.dBeta, lr); this.norm2.updateGradients(grads.norm2Grads.dGamma, grads.norm2Grads.dBeta, lr); }
}

class Transformer {
  constructor({ dModel = 32, nHeads = 4, nLayers = 2, dFF = 64, maxSeqLen = 100, outputDim = 1, seed = 42 }) { this.dModel = dModel; this.nHeads = nHeads; this.nLayers = nLayers; this.dFF = dFF; this.maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen; this.outputDim = outputDim; this.blocks = []; for (let i = 0; i < nLayers; i++) this.blocks.push(new TransformerBlock(dModel, nHeads, dFF, seed + i * 100)); this.Wout = xavier(dModel, outputDim, seed + 999); this.bout = new Float64Array(outputDim); this.PE = positionalEncoding(maxSeqLen, dModel); this.Win = null; this.bin = null; }
  _ensureInputProjection(inputDim) { if (!this.Win || this.Win.length !== this.dModel || this.Win[0].length !== inputDim) { this.Win = xavier(inputDim, this.dModel); this.bin = new Float64Array(this.dModel); } }
  forward(X, mask = null) { const n = X.length; this._ensureInputProjection(X[0].length); const projected = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dModel)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let o = 0; o < this.dModel; o++) { let sum = this.bin[o]; for (let j = 0; j < X[i].length; j++) sum += X[i][j] * this.Win[o][j]; projected[i][o] = sum; } for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) projected[i][j] += this.PE[i][j]; let current = projected; const blockCaches = []; for (const block of this.blocks) { const { output, cache } = block.forward(current, mask); blockCaches.push(cache); current = output; } const pooled = new Float64Array(this.dModel); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) pooled[j] += current[i][j] / n; const output = new Float64Array(this.outputDim); for (let o = 0; o < this.outputDim; o++) { output[o] = this.bout[o]; for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) output[o] += pooled[j] * this.Wout[o][j]; } return { output: Array.from(output), cache: { X, projected, current, blockCaches, pooled, n } }; }
  backward(dOutput, cache) { const { n, blockCaches, pooled } = cache; const dPooled = new Float64Array(this.dModel); const dWout = this.Wout.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const dbout = new Float64Array(this.outputDim); for (let o = 0; o < this.outputDim; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) dWout[o][j] += dOutput[o] * pooled[j]; dbout[o] += dOutput[o]; } for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < this.outputDim; o++) sum += dOutput[o] * this.Wout[o][j]; dPooled[j] = sum; } let dCurrent = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Float64Array(this.dModel)); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) dCurrent[i][j] = dPooled[j] / n; const blockGrads = []; for (let i = this.blocks.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const grads = this.blocks[i].backward(dCurrent, blockCaches[i]); blockGrads.unshift(grads); dCurrent = grads.dX; } return { dWout, dbout, blockGrads }; }
  updateGradients(grads, lr = 0.001) { const { dWout, dbout, blockGrads } = grads; for (let o = 0; o < this.outputDim; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < this.dModel; j++) this.Wout[o][j] -= lr * dWout[o][j]; this.bout[o] -= lr * dbout[o]; } for (let i = 0; i < this.blocks.length; i++) this.blocks[i].updateGradients(blockGrads[i], lr); }
  fit(sequences, targets, { epochs = 20, lr = 0.001, verbose = false } = {}) { const history = []; for (let epoch = 0; epoch < epochs; epoch++) { let totalLoss = 0; for (let s = 0; s < sequences.length; s++) { const X = sequences[s]; const y = targets[s]; const { output, cache } = this.forward(X); let loss = 0; const dOutput = new Float64Array(this.outputDim); for (let o = 0; o < this.outputDim; o++) { loss += (output[o] - y[o]) ** 2; dOutput[o] = 2 * (output[o] - y[o]); } totalLoss += loss; const grads = this.backward(dOutput, cache); this.updateGradients(grads, lr); } const avgLoss = totalLoss / sequences.length; history.push({ epoch, loss: avgLoss }); if (verbose && epoch % 5 === 0) console.log(`Epoch ${epoch}, loss: ${avgLoss.toFixed(6)}`); } return { history, finalLoss: history[history.length - 1].loss }; }
  predict(X) { return this.forward(X).output; }
}

function crucixTransformer(history, { seqLen = 10, epochs = 20, verbose = false } = {}) { const vix = history.map(h => h.fred?.vix || 20); const hySpread = history.map(h => h.fred?.hySpread || 3); const conflicts = history.map(h => h.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length || 0); const sanctions = history.map(h => h.sanctions?.count || 0); const oil = history.map(h => h.energy?.oilPrice || 70); if (vix.length < seqLen + 20) return { error: 'Мало данных', count: vix.length }; const sequences = []; const targets = []; for (let i = 0; i < vix.length - seqLen - 1; i++) { const seq = []; for (let t = 0; t < seqLen; t++) seq.push([vix[i + t] / 50, hySpread[i + t] / 10, conflicts[i + t] / 20, sanctions[i + t] / 5, oil[i + t] / 120]); sequences.push(seq); targets.push([vix[i + seqLen] / 50]); } const model = new Transformer({ dModel: 32, nHeads: 4, nLayers: 2, dFF: 64, maxSeqLen: seqLen, outputDim: 1 }); const trainResult = model.fit(sequences.slice(0, -5), targets.slice(0, -5), { epochs, lr: 0.001, verbose }); const lastSeq = sequences[sequences.length - 1]; const prediction = model.predict(lastSeq); return { prediction: prediction[0] * 50, predictedVix: prediction[0] * 50, currentVix: vix[vix.length - 1], trainingLoss: trainResult.finalLoss, history: trainResult.history, config: { dModel: 32, nHeads: 4, nLayers: 2, seqLen } }; }

export { Transformer, TransformerBlock, MultiHeadAttention, LayerNorm, FeedForward, positionalEncoding, crucixTransformer };

// apis/predict/models/vae.mjs
// Variational Autoencoder (Kingma & Welling, 2013)

function xavier(rows, cols, seed = 42) { const scale = Math.sqrt(2 / (rows + cols)); const W = []; let s = seed; const rand = () => { s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; return s / 233280; }; for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) { const col = new Float64Array(rows); for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { const u1 = rand(), u2 = rand(); const z = Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1 + 1e-10)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2); col[j] = z * scale; } W.push(col); } return W; }
function gaussianSample() { const u1 = Math.random(), u2 = Math.random(); return Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1 + 1e-10)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2); }
function euclideanDist(a, b) { let s = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += (a[i] - b[i]) ** 2; return Math.sqrt(s); }

class DenseLayer {
  constructor(inDim, outDim, activation = 'linear', seed = 42) { this.inDim = inDim; this.outDim = outDim; this.activation = activation; this.W = xavier(inDim, outDim, seed); this.b = new Float64Array(outDim).fill(0); this.mW = this.W.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); this.vW = this.W.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); this.mb = new Float64Array(outDim); this.vb = new Float64Array(outDim); this.t = 0; }
  forward(x) { const z = new Float64Array(this.outDim); for (let o = 0; o < this.outDim; o++) { let sum = this.b[o]; for (let i = 0; i < this.inDim; i++) sum += x[i] * this.W[o][i]; z[o] = sum; } let a; if (this.activation === 'relu') a = z.map(v => Math.max(0, v)); else if (this.activation === 'sigmoid') a = z.map(v => 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-v))); else if (this.activation === 'tanh') a = z.map(v => Math.tanh(v)); else a = Array.from(z); return { z, a, input: x }; }
  backward(dA, cache) { const { z, a, input } = cache; const dz = new Float64Array(this.outDim); for (let o = 0; o < this.outDim; o++) { if (this.activation === 'relu') dz[o] = z[o] > 0 ? dA[o] : 0; else if (this.activation === 'sigmoid') dz[o] = dA[o] * a[o] * (1 - a[o]); else if (this.activation === 'tanh') dz[o] = dA[o] * (1 - a[o] * a[o]); else dz[o] = dA[o]; } const dW = this.W.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)); const db = new Float64Array(this.outDim); for (let o = 0; o < this.outDim; o++) { for (let i = 0; i < this.inDim; i++) dW[o][i] = dz[o] * input[i]; db[o] = dz[o]; } const dInput = new Float64Array(this.inDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.inDim; i++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < this.outDim; o++) sum += dz[o] * this.W[o][i]; dInput[i] = sum; } return { dInput, dW, db }; }
  update(dW, db, lr = 0.001, beta1 = 0.9, beta2 = 0.999, eps = 1e-8) { this.t++; for (let o = 0; o < this.outDim; o++) { for (let i = 0; i < this.inDim; i++) { this.mW[o][i] = beta1 * this.mW[o][i] + (1 - beta1) * dW[o][i]; this.vW[o][i] = beta2 * this.vW[o][i] + (1 - beta2) * dW[o][i] * dW[o][i]; const mHat = this.mW[o][i] / (1 - Math.pow(beta1, this.t)); const vHat = this.vW[o][i] / (1 - Math.pow(beta2, this.t)); this.W[o][i] -= lr * mHat / (Math.sqrt(vHat) + eps); } this.mb[o] = beta1 * this.mb[o] + (1 - beta1) * db[o]; this.vb[o] = beta2 * this.vb[o] + (1 - beta2) * db[o] * db[o]; const mHat = this.mb[o] / (1 - Math.pow(beta1, this.t)); const vHat = this.vb[o] / (1 - Math.pow(beta2, this.t)); this.b[o] -= lr * mHat / (Math.sqrt(vHat) + eps); } }
}

class VAE {
  constructor({ inputDim = 10, encoderDims = [32, 16], latentDim = 4, decoderDims = [16, 32], seed = 42 }) { this.inputDim = inputDim; this.encoderDims = encoderDims; this.latentDim = latentDim; this.decoderDims = decoderDims; this.encoderLayers = []; let prevDim = inputDim; for (let i = 0; i < encoderDims.length; i++) { this.encoderLayers.push(new DenseLayer(prevDim, encoderDims[i], 'relu', seed + i * 10)); prevDim = encoderDims[i]; } this.muLayer = new DenseLayer(prevDim, latentDim, 'linear', seed + 100); this.logvarLayer = new DenseLayer(prevDim, latentDim, 'linear', seed + 110); this.decoderLayers = []; prevDim = latentDim; for (let i = 0; i < decoderDims.length; i++) { this.decoderLayers.push(new DenseLayer(prevDim, decoderDims[i], 'relu', seed + 200 + i * 10)); prevDim = decoderDims[i]; } this.outputLayer = new DenseLayer(prevDim, inputDim, 'linear', seed + 300); }
  encode(x) { let h = x; const encoderCaches = []; for (const layer of this.encoderLayers) { const result = layer.forward(h); encoderCaches.push(result); h = result.a; } const muResult = this.muLayer.forward(h); const logvarResult = this.logvarLayer.forward(h); return { mu: muResult.a, logvar: logvarResult.a, hidden: h, caches: { encoder: encoderCaches, mu: muResult, logvar: logvarResult } }; }
  reparameterize(mu, logvar) { const sigma = logvar.map(lv => Math.exp(0.5 * lv)); const epsilon = Array.from({ length: this.latentDim }, () => gaussianSample()); const z = mu.map((m, i) => m + sigma[i] * epsilon[i]); return { z, sigma, epsilon }; }
  decode(z) { let h = z; const decoderCaches = []; for (const layer of this.decoderLayers) { const result = layer.forward(h); decoderCaches.push(result); h = result.a; } const outputResult = this.outputLayer.forward(h); return { output: outputResult.a, caches: { decoder: decoderCaches, output: outputResult } }; }
  forward(x) { const enc = this.encode(x); const { z, sigma, epsilon } = this.reparameterize(enc.mu, enc.logvar); const dec = this.decode(z); let reconLoss = 0; for (let i = 0; i < this.inputDim; i++) reconLoss += (dec.output[i] - x[i]) ** 2; reconLoss /= this.inputDim; let klLoss = 0; for (let i = 0; i < this.latentDim; i++) klLoss += -0.5 * (1 + enc.logvar[i] - enc.mu[i] ** 2 - Math.exp(enc.logvar[i])); const totalLoss = reconLoss + klLoss; return { reconstruction: dec.output, mu: enc.mu, logvar: enc.logvar, z, loss: { reconLoss, klLoss, totalLoss }, caches: { encoder: enc.caches, decoder: dec.caches, z, mu: enc.mu, logvar: enc.logvar, sigma, epsilon, input: x } }; }
  backward(forwardResult) { const { caches } = forwardResult; const { input, z, mu, logvar, sigma, epsilon } = caches; const dOutput = new Float64Array(this.inputDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.inputDim; i++) dOutput[i] = 2 * (forwardResult.reconstruction[i] - input[i]) / this.inputDim; const decoderGrads = []; let dH = dOutput; const outputGrads = this.outputLayer.backward(dH, caches.decoder.output); dH = outputGrads.dInput; for (let i = this.decoderLayers.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const grads = this.decoderLayers[i].backward(dH, caches.decoder.decoder[i]); decoderGrads.unshift(grads); dH = grads.dInput; } const dZ = dH; const dMu = new Float64Array(this.latentDim); const dLogvarReparam = new Float64Array(this.latentDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.latentDim; i++) { dMu[i] = dZ[i]; dLogvarReparam[i] = 0.5 * sigma[i] * epsilon[i] * dZ[i]; } const dMuKL = new Float64Array(this.latentDim); const dLogvarKL = new Float64Array(this.latentDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.latentDim; i++) { dMuKL[i] = mu[i]; dLogvarKL[i] = 0.5 * (1 - Math.exp(logvar[i])); } const dMuTotal = dMu.map((v, i) => v + dMuKL[i]); const dLogvarTotal = dLogvarReparam.map((v, i) => v + dLogvarKL[i]); const muGrads = this.muLayer.backward(dMuTotal, caches.encoder.mu); const logvarGrads = this.logvarLayer.backward(dLogvarTotal, caches.encoder.logvar); const dHidden = new Float64Array(muGrads.dInput.length); for (let i = 0; i < dHidden.length; i++) dHidden[i] = muGrads.dInput[i] + logvarGrads.dInput[i]; const encoderGrads = []; dH = dHidden; for (let i = this.encoderLayers.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const grads = this.encoderLayers[i].backward(dH, caches.encoder.encoder[i]); encoderGrads.unshift(grads); dH = grads.dInput; } return { encoderGrads, muGrads, logvarGrads, decoderGrads, outputGrads }; }
  updateWeights(grads, lr = 0.001) { for (let i = 0; i < this.encoderLayers.length; i++) this.encoderLayers[i].update(grads.encoderGrads[i].dW, grads.encoderGrads[i].db, lr); this.muLayer.update(grads.muGrads.dW, grads.muGrads.db, lr); this.logvarLayer.update(grads.logvarGrads.dW, grads.logvarGrads.db, lr); for (let i = 0; i < this.decoderLayers.length; i++) this.decoderLayers[i].update(grads.decoderGrads[i].dW, grads.decoderGrads[i].db, lr); this.outputLayer.update(grads.outputGrads.dW, grads.outputGrads.db, lr); }
  fit(X, { epochs = 100, batchSize = 16, lr = 0.001, beta = 1.0, verbose = false } = {}) { const n = X.length; const history = []; for (let epoch = 0; epoch < epochs; epoch++) { const indices = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i); for (let i = n - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1)); [indices[i], indices[j]] = [indices[j], indices[i]]; } let epochRecon = 0, epochKL = 0, batchCount = 0; for (let start = 0; start < n; start += batchSize) { const batchIdx = indices.slice(start, start + batchSize); let batchRecon = 0, batchKL = 0; for (const idx of batchIdx) { const x = X[idx]; const fwd = this.forward(x); batchRecon += fwd.loss.reconLoss; batchKL += beta * fwd.loss.klLoss; const grads = this.backward(fwd); this.updateWeights(grads, lr / batchIdx.length); } epochRecon += batchRecon / batchIdx.length; epochKL += batchKL / batchIdx.length; batchCount++; } const avgRecon = epochRecon / batchCount; const avgKL = epochKL / batchCount; const totalLoss = avgRecon + avgKL; history.push({ epoch, reconLoss: avgRecon, klLoss: avgKL, totalLoss }); if (verbose && epoch % 10 === 0) console.log(`Epoch ${epoch}: recon=${avgRecon.toFixed(5)}, kl=${avgKL.toFixed(5)}`); } return { history, finalLoss: history[history.length - 1].totalLoss }; }
  encode_only(x) { return this.encode(x).mu; }
  generate(z = null) { const latent = z || Array.from({ length: this.latentDim }, () => gaussianSample()); const dec = this.decode(latent); return { sample: dec.output, latent }; }
  interpolate(x1, x2, steps = 5) { const z1 = this.encode_only(x1); const z2 = this.encode_only(x2); const results = []; for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) { const t = s / steps; const z = z1.map((v, i) => v * (1 - t) + z2[i] * t); const dec = this.decode(z); results.push({ t, sample: dec.output, latent: z }); } return results; }
}

function clusterLatentSpace(latentSpace, k = 3) { if (latentSpace.length < k) return []; const centroids = [latentSpace[Math.floor(Math.random() * latentSpace.length)]]; while (centroids.length < k) { const distances = latentSpace.map(x => Math.min(...centroids.map(c => euclideanDist(x, c)))); const sum = distances.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); let r = Math.random() * sum; for (let i = 0; i < latentSpace.length; i++) { r -= distances[i]; if (r <= 0) { centroids.push(latentSpace[i]); break; } } } let assignments = new Array(latentSpace.length).fill(0); for (let iter = 0; iter < 20; iter++) { assignments = latentSpace.map(x => { let best = 0, bestDist = Infinity; for (let c = 0; c < centroids.length; c++) { const d = euclideanDist(x, centroids[c]); if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = c; } } return best; }); for (let c = 0; c < k; c++) { const members = latentSpace.filter((_, i) => assignments[i] === c); if (members.length === 0) continue; for (let d = 0; d < centroids[c].length; d++) centroids[c][d] = members.reduce((s, m) => s + m[d], 0) / members.length; } } const clusterSizes = new Array(k).fill(0); for (const a of assignments) clusterSizes[a]++; return { centroids, assignments, sizes: clusterSizes, distribution: clusterSizes.map(s => s / latentSpace.length) }; }

function crucixVAE(history, { epochs = 50, latentDim = 4 } = {}) { const X = []; for (const h of history) X.push([(h.fred?.vix || 20) / 50, (h.fred?.hySpread || 3) / 10, (h.fred?.treasury10y || 4) / 6, (h.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length || 0) / 20, (h.sanctions?.count || 0) / 5, (h.energy?.oilPrice || 70) / 120, (h.gold?.price || 1900) / 2200, (h.dxy?.value || 100) / 110, h.radiation ? Math.max(...Object.values(h.radiation).map(s => s.cpm || 0)) / 200 : 0, (h.delta?.newAlerts || 0) / 10]); if (X.length < 30) return { error: 'Мало данных', count: X.length }; const model = new VAE({ inputDim: 10, encoderDims: [32, 16], latentDim, decoderDims: [16, 32] }); const trainResult = model.fit(X, { epochs, batchSize: 16, lr: 0.001 }); const latentSpace = X.map(x => model.encode_only(x)); const currentLatent = model.encode_only(X[X.length - 1]); const reconstruction = model.forward(X[X.length - 1]); const reconstructionErrors = X.map((x, i) => { const fwd = model.forward(x); let err = 0; for (let j = 0; j < x.length; j++) err += (fwd.reconstruction[j] - x[j]) ** 2; return Math.sqrt(err / x.length); }); const avgReconError = reconstructionErrors.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / reconstructionErrors.length; const scenarios = []; for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) scenarios.push(model.generate().sample); return { model, latentDim, latentSpace, currentLatent, reconstruction: reconstruction.reconstruction, reconstructionError: avgReconError, scenarios, trainingLoss: trainResult.finalLoss, interpretation: avgReconError < 0.1 ? 'система хорошо вписана в латентное пространство' : 'высокая ошибка реконструкции — необычное состояние', latentClusters: clusterLatentSpace(latentSpace, 3) }; }

export { VAE, DenseLayer, crucixVAE, clusterLatentSpace };

// apis/predict/models/diffusion.mjs
// Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (Ho et al., 2020)

function linearBetaSchedule(T = 1000, betaStart = 1e-4, betaEnd = 0.02) { const betas = []; for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) betas.push(betaStart + (betaEnd - betaStart) * (t / (T - 1))); const alphas = betas.map(b => 1 - b); const alphaBars = []; let prod = 1; for (const a of alphas) { prod *= a; alphaBars.push(prod); } return { betas, alphas, alphaBars }; }
function cosineBetaSchedule(T = 1000, s = 0.008) { const betas = []; const alphaBars = []; const f = t => Math.cos(((t / T + s) / (1 + s)) * Math.PI * 0.5) ** 2; const f0 = f(0); for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) alphaBars.push(f(t) / f0); for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) { if (t === 0) betas.push(1 - alphaBars[0]); else betas.push(Math.min(0.999, 1 - alphaBars[t] / alphaBars[t - 1])); } return { betas, alphaBars, alphas: betas.map(b => 1 - b) }; }
function xavier(rows, cols, seed) { const scale = Math.sqrt(2 / (rows + cols)); const W = []; let s = seed || 42; const rand = () => { s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; return s / 233280; }; for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) { const col = new Float64Array(rows); for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) { const u1 = rand(), u2 = rand(); const z = Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1 + 1e-10)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2); col[j] = z * scale; } W.push(col); } return W; }
function timeEmbedding(t, dim) { const emb = new Float64Array(dim); for (let i = 0; i < dim; i += 2) { const freq = Math.exp(-Math.log(10000) * i / dim); emb[i] = Math.sin(t * freq); if (i + 1 < dim) emb[i + 1] = Math.cos(t * freq); } return emb; }
function gaussianSample() { const u1 = Math.random(), u2 = Math.random(); return Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1 + 1e-10)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2); }

class DenoiseNet {
  constructor({ inputDim = 10, timeDim = 32, hiddenDim = 128, nLayers = 3, seed = 42 }) { this.inputDim = inputDim; this.timeDim = timeDim; this.hiddenDim = hiddenDim; this.nLayers = nLayers; const layers = []; let prevDim = inputDim + timeDim; for (let i = 0; i < nLayers; i++) { layers.push({ W: xavier(prevDim, hiddenDim, seed + i * 10), b: new Float64Array(hiddenDim), inDim: prevDim, outDim: hiddenDim }); prevDim = hiddenDim; } layers.push({ W: xavier(prevDim, inputDim, seed + 999), b: new Float64Array(inputDim), inDim: prevDim, outDim: inputDim }); this.layers = layers; }
  forward(x_t, t) { const tEmb = timeEmbedding(t, this.timeDim); let h = new Float64Array(this.inputDim + this.timeDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.inputDim; i++) h[i] = x_t[i]; for (let i = 0; i < this.timeDim; i++) h[this.inputDim + i] = tEmb[i]; const activations = [h]; for (let i = 0; i < this.layers.length; i++) { const layer = this.layers[i]; const out = new Float64Array(layer.outDim); for (let o = 0; o < layer.outDim; o++) { let sum = layer.b[o]; for (let j = 0; j < layer.inDim; j++) sum += h[j] * layer.W[o][j]; out[o] = sum; } if (i < this.layers.length - 1) for (let o = 0; o < layer.outDim; o++) out[o] = Math.max(0, out[o]); h = out; activations.push(h); } return { output: h, activations }; }
  backward(dOutput, activations) { const grads = this.layers.map(layer => ({ dW: layer.W.map(row => new Float64Array(row.length)), db: new Float64Array(layer.outDim) })); let dH = dOutput; for (let l = this.layers.length - 1; l >= 0; l--) { const layer = this.layers[l]; const input = activations[l]; for (let o = 0; o < layer.outDim; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < layer.inDim; j++) grads[l].dW[o][j] = dH[o] * input[j]; grads[l].db[o] = dH[o]; } const dInput = new Float64Array(layer.inDim); for (let j = 0; j < layer.inDim; j++) { let sum = 0; for (let o = 0; o < layer.outDim; o++) sum += dH[o] * layer.W[o][j]; dInput[j] = sum; } if (l < this.layers.length - 1 && l > 0) { const prevActivation = activations[l]; for (let j = 0; j < dInput.length; j++) if (prevActivation[j] <= 0) dInput[j] = 0; } dH = dInput; } return grads; }
  update(grads, lr = 0.001) { for (let l = 0; l < this.layers.length; l++) { const layer = this.layers[l]; for (let o = 0; o < layer.outDim; o++) { for (let j = 0; j < layer.inDim; j++) layer.W[o][j] -= lr * grads[l].dW[o][j]; layer.b[o] -= lr * grads[l].db[o]; } } }
}

class DDPM {
  constructor({ dataDim = 10, T = 200, hiddenDim = 128, nLayers = 3, schedule = 'linear', seed = 42 }) { this.dataDim = dataDim; this.T = T; const { betas, alphas, alphaBars } = schedule === 'cosine' ? cosineBetaSchedule(T) : linearBetaSchedule(T); this.betas = betas; this.alphas = alphas; this.alphaBars = alphaBars; this.sigmas = betas.map(b => Math.sqrt(b)); this.net = new DenoiseNet({ inputDim: dataDim, timeDim: 32, hiddenDim, nLayers, seed }); }
  q_sample(x0, t, noise = null) { const alphaBar = this.alphaBars[t]; const sqrtAlphaBar = Math.sqrt(alphaBar); const sqrtOneMinusAlphaBar = Math.sqrt(1 - alphaBar); const eps = noise || Array.from({ length: this.dataDim }, () => gaussianSample()); const x_t = new Float64Array(this.dataDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.dataDim; i++) x_t[i] = sqrtAlphaBar * x0[i] + sqrtOneMinusAlphaBar * eps[i]; return { x_t, noise: eps }; }
  p_sample(x_t, t) { const { output: predictedNoise } = this.net.forward(x_t, t); const alpha = this.alphas[t]; const alphaBar = this.alphaBars[t]; const beta = this.betas[t]; const sqrtAlpha = Math.sqrt(alpha); const sqrtOneMinusAlphaBar = Math.sqrt(1 - alphaBar); const mu = new Float64Array(this.dataDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.dataDim; i++) mu[i] = (1 / sqrtAlpha) * (x_t[i] - (beta / sqrtOneMinusAlphaBar) * predictedNoise[i]); if (t > 0) { const sigma = this.sigmas[t]; const z = Array.from({ length: this.dataDim }, () => gaussianSample()); const x_prev = new Float64Array(this.dataDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.dataDim; i++) x_prev[i] = mu[i] + sigma * z[i]; return { x_prev, predictedNoise, mu }; } return { x_prev: mu, predictedNoise, mu }; }
  generate({ startFrom = null, returnTrajectory = false } = {}) { let x = startFrom || Array.from({ length: this.dataDim }, () => gaussianSample()); const trajectory = returnTrajectory ? [{ t: this.T, x: [...x] }] : []; for (let t = this.T - 1; t >= 0; t--) { const { x_prev } = this.p_sample(x, t); x = Array.from(x_prev); if (returnTrajectory && t % 10 === 0) trajectory.push({ t, x: [...x] }); } return { sample: x, trajectory }; }
  train_step(x0, lr = 0.001) { const t = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.T); const { x_t, noise } = this.q_sample(x0, t); const { output: predictedNoise, activations } = this.net.forward(x_t, t); let loss = 0; const dOutput = new Float64Array(this.dataDim); for (let i = 0; i < this.dataDim; i++) { const diff = predictedNoise[i] - noise[i]; loss += diff * diff; dOutput[i] = 2 * diff / this.dataDim; } loss /= this.dataDim; const grads = this.net.backward(dOutput, activations); this.net.update(grads, lr); return { loss, t }; }
  fit(X, { epochs = 100, batchSize = 16, lr = 0.001, verbose = false } = {}) { const n = X.length; const history = []; for (let epoch = 0; epoch < epochs; epoch++) { const indices = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i); for (let i = n - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1)); [indices[i], indices[j]] = [indices[j], indices[i]]; } let epochLoss = 0, count = 0; for (let start = 0; start < n; start += batchSize) { const batch = indices.slice(start, start + batchSize); for (const idx of batch) { const { loss } = this.train_step(X[idx], lr); epochLoss += loss; count++; } } const avgLoss = epochLoss / count; history.push({ epoch, loss: avgLoss }); if (verbose && epoch % 10 === 0) console.log(`Epoch ${epoch}, loss: ${avgLoss.toFixed(6)}`); } return { history, finalLoss: history[history.length - 1].loss }; }
}

function crucixDiffusion(history, { epochs = 100, nScenarios = 10, T = 200 } = {}) { const X = []; for (const h of history) X.push([(h.fred?.vix || 20) / 50, (h.fred?.hySpread || 3) / 10, (h.fred?.treasury10y || 4) / 6, (h.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length || 0) / 20, (h.sanctions?.count || 0) / 5, (h.energy?.oilPrice || 70) / 120, (h.gold?.price || 1900) / 2200, (h.dxy?.value || 100) / 110, h.radiation ? Math.max(...Object.values(h.radiation).map(s => s.cpm || 0)) / 200 : 0, (h.delta?.newAlerts || 0) / 10]); if (X.length < 30) return { error: 'Мало данных', count: X.length }; const model = new DDPM({ dataDim: 10, T, hiddenDim: 128, nLayers: 3, schedule: 'cosine' }); const trainResult = model.fit(X, { epochs, batchSize: 16, lr: 0.001 }); const scenarios = []; for (let i = 0; i < nScenarios; i++) { const { sample } = model.generate(); scenarios.push({ vix: sample[0] * 50, hySpread: sample[1] * 10, treasury10y: sample[2] * 6, conflicts: sample[3] * 20, sanctions: sample[4] * 5, oil: sample[5] * 120, gold: sample[6] * 2200, dxy: sample[7] * 110 }); } const vixValues = scenarios.map(s => s.vix).sort((a, b) => a - b); const conflictValues = scenarios.map(s => s.conflicts).sort((a, b) => a - b); return { scenarios, trainingLoss: trainResult.finalLoss, model, statistics: { vix: { mean: vixValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vixValues.length, p05: vixValues[Math.floor(vixValues.length * 0.05)], p50: vixValues[Math.floor(vixValues.length * 0.50)], p95: vixValues[Math.floor(vixValues.length * 0.95)], min: vixValues[0], max: vixValues[vixValues.length - 1] }, conflicts: { mean: conflictValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / conflictValues.length, p50: conflictValues[Math.floor(conflictValues.length * 0.50)], p95: conflictValues[Math.floor(conflictValues.length * 0.95)] } }, tailRisk: { probabilityVixAbove30: vixValues.filter(v => v > 30).length / vixValues.length, probabilityVixAbove40: vixValues.filter(v => v > 40).length / vixValues.length, probabilityConflictsAbove15: conflictValues.filter(c => c > 15).length / conflictValues.length } }; }

export { DDPM, DenoiseNet, linearBetaSchedule, cosineBetaSchedule, timeEmbedding, crucixDiffusion };
