// apis/predict/models/bayesnet.mjs
// Bayesian Networks — Pearl (1988) Probabilistic Reasoning in Intelligent Systems
//
// Дискретные байесовские сети: DAG + conditional probability tables.
// Три режима inference: exact enumeration, forward sampling, likelihood weighting.
// Обучение: MLE с Laplace smoothing + байесовское (Dirichlet prior).
// Метрика: DIC (Spiegelhalter et al. 2002) для сравнения сетей.
//
// Теоретическая основа:
//   - Pearl, J. (1988). "Probabilistic Reasoning in Intelligent Systems:
//     Networks of Plausible Inference". Morgan Kaufmann.
//   - Lauritzen, S. L., & Spiegelhalter, D. J. (1988). "Local Computations
//     with Probabilities on Graphical Structures and Their Application to
//     Expert Systems". JRSS B.
//   - Heckerman, D. (1998). "A Tutorial on Learning with Bayesian Networks".
//     NATO ASI Series.
//   - Spiegelhalter, D. J., et al. (2002). "Bayesian measures of model
//     complexity and fit". JRSS B.
//   - Koller, D., & Friedman, N. (2009). "Probabilistic Graphical Models:
//     Principles and Techniques". MIT Press.
//
// Версия: 6.0.0

import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// УТИЛИТЫ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function ensureDir(dir) {
  if (!existsSync(dir)) mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
}

function saveJSON(fp, data) {
  ensureDir(dirname(fp));
  writeFileSync(fp, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

function mean(arr) {
  if (arr.length === 0) return 0;
  return arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length;
}

function std(arr, ddof = 1) {
  const n = arr.length;
  if (n <= ddof) return 0;
  const m = mean(arr);
  return Math.sqrt(arr.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - m) ** 2, 0) / (n - ddof));
}

/**
 * Сэмпл из гамма-распределения (Marsaglia-Tsang)
 */
function gammaSample(shape, scale) {
  if (shape < 1) {
    return gammaSample(shape + 1, scale) * Math.pow(Math.random(), 1 / shape);
  }
  const d = shape - 1 / 3;
  const c = 1 / Math.sqrt(9 * d);
  let x, v;
  do {
    do { x = gaussianRandom(0, 1); v = 1 + c * x; } while (v <= 0);
    v = v * v * v;
  } while (Math.random() >= 1 - 0.0331 * Math.pow(x, 4)
    && Math.log(Math.random()) >= 0.5 * x * x + d * (1 - v + Math.log(v)));
  return d * scale * v;
}

function gaussianRandom(meanVal = 0, sd = 1) {
  const u1 = Math.random();
  const u2 = Math.random();
  const z = Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1 + 1e-10)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2);
  return meanVal + z * sd;
}

/**
 * Сэмпл из Dirichlet(alpha).
 */
function dirichletSample(alphas) {
  const gammas = alphas.map(a => gammaSample(a, 1));
  const sum = gammas.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  return gammas.map(g => g / sum);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 1. BAYESIAN NETWORK — ядро
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Узел байесовской сети.
 * Хранит: имя, состояния, родителей, CPT.
 * CPT: массив длины K_parentCombinations, каждый элемент — массив длины K
 * (распределение по состояниям узла при данной комбинации родителей).
 */
class BayesNode {
  constructor(name, states, parents = []) {
    this.name = name;
    this.states = states;
    this.parents = parents;
    this.K = states.length;

    // Инициализация CPT равномерным распределением
    const numParentCombos = this._estimateParentCombos();
    this.cpt = Array.from({ length: numParentCombos }, () =>
      new Array(this.K).fill(1 / this.K)
    );

    // Для обучения: счётчики (Dirichlet)
    this.counts = Array.from({ length: numParentCombos }, () =>
      new Array(this.K).fill(0)
    );
  }

  _estimateParentCombos() {
    // Временная оценка, будет уточнена при добавлении в сеть
    return Math.pow(2, this.parents.length);
  }

  /**
   * Установка числа комбинаций родителей (вызывается сетью).
   */
  setParentCombos(num) {
    if (this.cpt.length === num) return;
    this.cpt = Array.from({ length: num }, () =>
      new Array(this.K).fill(1 / this.K)
    );
    this.counts = Array.from({ length: num }, () =>
      new Array(this.K).fill(0)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Получить распределение узла при заданной комбинации родителей.
   */
  getDistribution(parentIdx) {
    return this.cpt[parentIdx] || new Array(this.K).fill(1 / this.K);
  }
}

/**
 * Байесовская сеть с дискретными узлами.
 */
class BayesianNetwork {
  constructor(name = 'bn') {
    this.name = name;
    this.nodes = new Map(); // name → BayesNode
    this.edges = [];        // [{ from, to }]
    this._topoOrder = null;
    this._parentCombos = new Map(); // name → количество комбинаций
  }

  /**
   * Добавление узла.
   * @param {string} name — имя узла
   * @param {string[]} states — список состояний (например, ['low', 'medium', 'high'])
   * @param {string[]} parents — имена родителей
   */
  addNode(name, states, parents = []) {
    // Проверка: родители должны быть добавлены
    for (const p of parents) {
      if (!this.nodes.has(p)) {
        throw new Error(`Parent '${p}' for node '${name}' not found. Add parents first.`);
      }
    }

    const node = new BayesNode(name, states, [...parents]);

    // Вычисляем количество комбинаций родителей
    let numCombos = 1;
    for (const p of parents) {
      numCombos *= this.nodes.get(p).K;
    }
    node.setParentCombos(numCombos);
    this._parentCombos.set(name, numCombos);

    this.nodes.set(name, node);

    for (const p of parents) {
      this.edges.push({ from: p, to: name });
    }

    // Инвалидация топологического порядка
    this._topoOrder = null;

    return this;
  }

  /**
   * Установка CPT вручную.
   * @param {string} name
   * @param {number[][]} cpt — массив распределений
   */
  setCPT(name, cpt) {
    const node = this.nodes.get(name);
    if (!node) throw new Error(`Node '${name}' not found`);
    if (cpt.length !== node.cpt.length) {
      throw new Error(`CPT for '${name}' should have ${node.cpt.length} entries, got ${cpt.length}`);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < cpt.length; i++) {
      const sum = cpt[i].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
      if (Math.abs(sum - 1) > 1e-6) {
        throw new Error(`CPT row ${i} for '${name}' sums to ${sum}, expected 1`);
      }
    }
    node.cpt = cpt.map(row => [...row]);
    return this;
  }

  /**
   * Топологическая сортировка (Kahn's algorithm).
   */
  _topologicalSort() {
    if (this._topoOrder) return this._topoOrder;

    const indeg = new Map();
    const adj = new Map();
    for (const name of this.nodes.keys()) {
      indeg.set(name, 0);
      adj.set(name, []);
    }
    for (const e of this.edges) {
      adj.get(e.from).push(e.to);
      indeg.set(e.to, indeg.get(e.to) + 1);
    }

    const queue = [...this.nodes.keys()].filter(n => indeg.get(n) === 0);
    const sorted = [];
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const u = queue.shift();
      sorted.push(u);
      for (const v of adj.get(u)) {
        indeg.set(v, indeg.get(v) - 1);
        if (indeg.get(v) === 0) queue.push(v);
      }
    }

    if (sorted.length !== this.nodes.size) {
      throw new Error('Network contains a cycle — not a valid DAG');
    }

    this._topoOrder = sorted;
    return sorted;
  }

  /**
   * Индекс комбинации родителей для узла при заданном assignment.
   * Используем смешанную систему счисления, где младший разряд — первый родитель.
   */
  _parentIndex(node, assignment) {
    if (node.parents.length === 0) return 0;
    let idx = 0;
    let mult = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < node.parents.length; i++) {
      const pName = node.parents[i];
      const pNode = this.nodes.get(pName);
      const pState = assignment[pName] || 0;
      idx += pState * mult;
      mult *= pNode.K;
    }
    return idx;
  }

  /**
   * Совместная вероятность одного полного assignment.
   */
  _jointProbability(assignment) {
    let prob = 1;
    for (const [name, node] of this.nodes) {
      const state = assignment[name];
      if (state === undefined) return 0;
      const pIdx = this._parentIndex(node, assignment);
      const dist = node.getDistribution(pIdx);
      prob *= dist[state] || 0;
      if (prob === 0) return 0;
    }
    return prob;
  }

  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // INFERENCE 1: EXACT ENUMERATION
  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  /**
   * Точный inference через полное перечисление.
   * Работает только для малых сетей (≤ 15 узлов).
   *
   * @param {string} target — узел, для которого запрашиваем распределение
   * @param {Object} evidence — { nodeName: stateIdx }
   * @returns {Object} — { target, states, probabilities }
   */
  exactInference(target, evidence = {}) {
    const targetNode = this.nodes.get(target);
    if (!targetNode) throw new Error(`Node '${target}' not found`);

    const totalCombos = [...this.nodes.values()].reduce((a, n) => a * n.K, 1);
    if (totalCombos > 1e7) {
      return {
        target,
        error: 'network_too_large_for_exact',
        estimatedCombos: totalCombos,
        hint: 'Use likelihoodWeighting() or forwardSampling() instead',
      };
    }

    const topoOrder = this._topologicalSort();
    const result = new Array(targetNode.K).fill(0);
    const assignment = {};

    const enumerate = (idx) => {
      if (idx === topoOrder.length) {
        const prob = this._jointProbability(assignment);
        if (prob > 0) {
          result[assignment[target]] += prob;
        }
        return;
      }
      const name = topoOrder[idx];
      const node = this.nodes.get(name);

      if (evidence[name] !== undefined) {
        assignment[name] = evidence[name];
        enumerate(idx + 1);
        delete assignment[name];
      } else {
        for (let s = 0; s < node.K; s++) {
          assignment[name] = s;
          enumerate(idx + 1);
        }
        delete assignment[name];
      }
    };

    enumerate(0);

    const sum = result.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const probabilities = sum > 0 ? result.map(p => p / sum) : result.map(() => 1 / targetNode.K);

    return {
      target,
      states: [...targetNode.states],
      probabilities,
      evidence,
      method: 'exact_enumeration',
    };
  }

  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // INFERENCE 2: FORWARD SAMPLING
  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  /**
   * Forward sampling (Monte Carlo).
   * Простой, но игнорирует evidence при генерации — многие сэмплы пропадают.
   */
  forwardSampling(target, evidence = {}, nSamples = 10000) {
    const targetNode = this.nodes.get(target);
    if (!targetNode) throw new Error(`Node '${target}' not found`);

    const topoOrder = this._topologicalSort();
    const counts = new Array(targetNode.K).fill(0);
    let accepted = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < nSamples; i++) {
      const assignment = {};
      let consistentWithEvidence = true;

      for (const name of topoOrder) {
        const node = this.nodes.get(name);

        // Если узел в evidence — фиксируем
        if (evidence[name] !== undefined) {
          assignment[name] = evidence[name];
          continue;
        }

        // Сэмпл из CPT
        const pIdx = this._parentIndex(node, assignment);
        const dist = node.getDistribution(pIdx);
        const r = Math.random();
        let cum = 0;
        let sampledState = node.K - 1;
        for (let k = 0; k < node.K; k++) {
          cum += dist[k];
          if (r < cum) { sampledState = k; break; }
        }
        assignment[name] = sampledState;
      }

      // Проверка consistency с evidence
      for (const [name, state] of Object.entries(evidence)) {
        if (assignment[name] !== state) {
          consistentWithEvidence = false;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (consistentWithEvidence) {
        counts[assignment[target]]++;
        accepted++;
      }
    }

    const probabilities = accepted > 0
      ? counts.map(c => c / accepted)
      : counts.map(() => 1 / targetNode.K);

    return {
      target,
      states: [...targetNode.states],
      probabilities,
      evidence,
      nSamples,
      acceptedSamples: accepted,
      acceptanceRate: accepted / nSamples,
      method: 'forward_sampling',
    };
  }

  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // INFERENCE 3: LIKELIHOOD WEIGHTING
  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  /**
   * Likelihood weighting — сэмплируем только не-evidence переменные,
   * взвешиваем на P(evidence | parents). Работает гораздо лучше forward sampling.
   */
  likelihoodWeighting(target, evidence = {}, nSamples = 10000) {
    const targetNode = this.nodes.get(target);
    if (!targetNode) throw new Error(`Node '${target}' not found`);

    const topoOrder = this._topologicalSort();
    const weightedCounts = new Array(targetNode.K).fill(0);
    let totalWeight = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < nSamples; i++) {
      const assignment = {};
      let weight = 1;

      for (const name of topoOrder) {
        const node = this.nodes.get(name);

        if (evidence[name] !== undefined) {
          assignment[name] = evidence[name];
          // Умножаем вес на P(evidence | parents)
          const pIdx = this._parentIndex(node, assignment);
          const dist = node.getDistribution(pIdx);
          weight *= dist[evidence[name]] || 1e-10;
        } else {
          const pIdx = this._parentIndex(node, assignment);
          const dist = node.getDistribution(pIdx);
          const r = Math.random();
          let cum = 0;
          let sampledState = node.K - 1;
          for (let k = 0; k < node.K; k++) {
            cum += dist[k];
            if (r < cum) { sampledState = k; break; }
          }
          assignment[name] = sampledState;
        }
      }

      if (weight > 0) {
        weightedCounts[assignment[target]] += weight;
        totalWeight += weight;
      }
    }

    const probabilities = totalWeight > 0
      ? weightedCounts.map(c => c / totalWeight)
      : weightedCounts.map(() => 1 / targetNode.K);

    return {
      target,
      states: [...targetNode.states],
      probabilities,
      evidence,
      nSamples,
      effectiveSampleSize: totalWeight > 0
        ? (totalWeight * totalWeight) / Math.max(weightedCounts.reduce((s, c) => s + c * c, 0), 1e-10)
        : 0,
      method: 'likelihood_weighting',
    };
  }

  /**
   * Универсальный inference: выбирает метод автоматически.
   */
  infer(target, evidence = {}, options = {}) {
    const nNodes = this.nodes.size;
    const totalCombos = [...this.nodes.values()].reduce((a, n) => a * n.K, 1);

    if (nNodes <= 15 && totalCombos <= 1e6) {
      return this.exactInference(target, evidence);
    }

    const nSamples = options.nSamples || 10000;
    if (Object.keys(evidence).length > 0) {
      return this.likelihoodWeighting(target, evidence, nSamples);
    }
    return this.forwardSampling(target, evidence, nSamples);
  }

  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // ОБУЧЕНИЕ CPT ИЗ ДАННЫХ
  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  /**
   * Обучение CPT из данных: MLE с Laplace smoothing.
   *
   * @param {Array<Object>} data — массив наблюдений { nodeName: stateIdx }
   * @param {number} alpha — параметр сглаживания (default 1)
   */
  learnFromDataMLE(data, alpha = 1) {
    // Сброс счётчиков
    for (const node of this.nodes.values()) {
      for (let i = 0; i < node.counts.length; i++) {
        for (let k = 0; k < node.K; k++) node.counts[i][k] = alpha;
      }
    }

    // Подсчёт
    for (const row of data) {
      for (const [name, node] of this.nodes) {
        const state = row[name];
        if (state === undefined || state < 0 || state >= node.K) continue;
        const pIdx = this._parentIndex(node, row);
        node.counts[pIdx][state]++;
      }
    }

    // Нормализация → CPT
    for (const node of this.nodes.values()) {
      for (let i = 0; i < node.counts.length; i++) {
        const sum = node.counts[i].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
        node.cpt[i] = node.counts[i].map(c => c / sum);
      }
    }

    return this;
  }

  /**
   * Байесовское обучение CPT: Dirichlet prior + наблюдения.
   * Полезно для малых выборок: prior stabilises.
   */
  learnFromDataBayesian(data, priorAlpha = 1) {
    return this.learnFromDataMLE(data, priorAlpha);
  }

  /**
   * Log-likelihood данных при текущей сети.
   */
  logLikelihood(data) {
    let ll = 0;
    for (const row of data) {
      const prob = this._jointProbability(row);
      if (prob > 0) {
        ll += Math.log(prob);
      } else {
        ll += Math.log(1e-10);
      }
    }
    return ll;
  }

  /**
   * DIC (Deviance Information Criterion):
   *   DIC = Dbar + pD
   *   Dbar = -2 · E[log L(θ)]
   *   pD = Dbar - D(θ̂)
   * Здесь — упрощённая версия через log-likelihood.
   */
  dic(data) {
    const ll = this.logLikelihood(data);
    const Dhat = -2 * ll;
    const nParams = [...this.nodes.values()].reduce((s, n) =>
      s + n.cpt.length * (n.K - 1), 0);
    const pD = nParams;
    return {
      dic: Dhat + 2 * pD,
      Dhat,
      pD,
      nParams,
      logLikelihood: ll,
      nData: data.length,
    };
  }

  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // СТРУКТУРНОЕ ОБУЧЕНИЕ (упрощённый жадный поиск)
  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  /**
   * Генерация всех возможных DAG для малого набора переменных.
   * Для N переменных — 2^(N·(N-1)/2) возможных структур.
   */
  static enumerateDAGs(variables, statesPerVar) {
    const n = variables.length;
    const possibleEdges = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
        possibleEdges.push([i, j]); // i → j
      }
    }

    const numEdges = possibleEdges.length;
    if (numEdges > 12) return { error: 'too_many_edges', numEdges };

    const dags = [];
    const maxMask = 1 << numEdges;
    for (let mask = 0; mask < maxMask; mask++) {
      const edges = [];
      for (let e = 0; e < numEdges; e++) {
        if (mask & (1 << e)) {
          edges.push(possibleEdges[e]);
        }
      }
      // Проверка ацикличности
      if (BayesianNetwork._isAcyclic(n, edges)) {
        dags.push(edges);
      }
    }
    return { dags, numDAGs: dags.length };
  }

  static _isAcyclic(n, edges) {
    const adj = Array.from({ length: n }, () => []);
    for (const [from, to] of edges) adj[from].push(to);

    const state = new Array(n).fill(0); // 0=white, 1=gray, 2=black
    const dfs = (u) => {
      state[u] = 1;
      for (const v of adj[u]) {
        if (state[v] === 1) return false;
        if (state[v] === 0 && !dfs(v)) return false;
      }
      state[u] = 2;
      return true;
    };

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      if (state[i] === 0 && !dfs(i)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ГОТОВЫЕ СЕТИ ДЛЯ CRUCIX
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Причинная сеть: конфликт → санкции → напряжение → VIX.
 */
function buildCrucixCausalNetwork() {
  const bn = new BayesianNetwork('crucix_causal');

  bn.addNode('conflict', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], []);
  bn.addNode('sanctions', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['conflict']);
  bn.addNode('tension', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['conflict', 'sanctions']);
  bn.addNode('vix', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['tension']);

  // Примерные CPT (будут обучены из данных)
  bn.setCPT('conflict', [
    [0.6, 0.3, 0.1],
  ]);
  bn.setCPT('sanctions', [
    [0.8, 0.18, 0.02],  // conflict=low
    [0.3, 0.55, 0.15],  // conflict=medium
    [0.05, 0.30, 0.65], // conflict=high
  ]);
  bn.setCPT('tension', [
    [0.85, 0.13, 0.02],  // conflict=low, sanctions=low
    [0.60, 0.35, 0.05],  // conflict=low, sanctions=medium
    [0.40, 0.45, 0.15],  // conflict=low, sanctions=high
    [0.50, 0.40, 0.10],  // conflict=medium, sanctions=low
    [0.30, 0.50, 0.20],  // conflict=medium, sanctions=medium
    [0.15, 0.40, 0.45],  // conflict=medium, sanctions=high
    [0.20, 0.40, 0.40],  // conflict=high, sanctions=low
    [0.10, 0.35, 0.55],  // conflict=high, sanctions=medium
    [0.02, 0.18, 0.80],  // conflict=high, sanctions=high
  ]);
  bn.setCPT('vix', [
    [0.75, 0.22, 0.03],  // tension=low
    [0.35, 0.50, 0.15],  // tension=medium
    [0.05, 0.25, 0.70],  // tension=high
  ]);

  return bn;
}

/**
 * Рыночная сеть: VIX → спреды → валюты → золото.
 */
function buildCrucixMarketNetwork() {
  const bn = new BayesianNetwork('crucix_market');

  bn.addNode('vix', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], []);
  bn.addNode('hySpread', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['vix']);
  bn.addNode('dxy', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['vix', 'hySpread']);
  bn.addNode('gold', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['vix', 'dxy']);

  bn.setCPT('vix', [
    [0.7, 0.25, 0.05],
  ]);
  bn.setCPT('hySpread', [
    [0.85, 0.13, 0.02],  // vix=low
    [0.40, 0.50, 0.10],  // vix=medium
    [0.10, 0.35, 0.55],  // vix=high
  ]);
  bn.setCPT('dxy', [
    [0.65, 0.30, 0.05],  // vix=low, hySpread=low
    [0.50, 0.40, 0.10],  // vix=low, hySpread=medium
    [0.30, 0.50, 0.20],  // vix=low, hySpread=high
    [0.40, 0.45, 0.15],  // vix=medium, hySpread=low
    [0.35, 0.45, 0.20],  // vix=medium, hySpread=medium
    [0.20, 0.45, 0.35],  // vix=medium, hySpread=high
    [0.25, 0.50, 0.25],  // vix=high, hySpread=low
    [0.15, 0.45, 0.40],  // vix=high, hySpread=medium
    [0.10, 0.35, 0.55],  // vix=high, hySpread=high
  ]);
  bn.setCPT('gold', [
    [0.55, 0.35, 0.10],  // vix=low, dxy=low
    [0.45, 0.45, 0.10],  // vix=low, dxy=medium
    [0.35, 0.50, 0.15],  // vix=low, dxy=high
    [0.35, 0.45, 0.20],  // vix=medium, dxy=low
    [0.25, 0.50, 0.25],  // vix=medium, dxy=medium
    [0.20, 0.45, 0.35],  // vix=medium, dxy=high
    [0.15, 0.40, 0.45],  // vix=high, dxy=low
    [0.10, 0.35, 0.55],  // vix=high, dxy=medium
    [0.05, 0.25, 0.70],  // vix=high, dxy=high
  ]);

  return bn;
}

  bn.addNode('escalationRisk', ['low', 'medium', 'high'], ['conflictCount', 'sanctionsPressure']);

// apis/predict/anomaly_detection.mjs
// Anomaly Detection для прогностического слоя Crucix
// Пять методов + ансамбль: Isolation Forest, LOF, Mahalanobis, One-Class SVM, DBSCAN
//
// Теоретическая основа:
//   Isolation Forest:
//     - Liu, F. T., Ting, K. M., & Zhou, Z. H. (2008). "Isolation Forest".
//       ICDM 2008.
//     - Liu, F. T., Ting, K. M., & Zhou, Z. H. (2012). "Isolation-Based
//       Anomaly Detection". ACM TKDD.
//
//   LOF (Local Outlier Factor):
//     - Breunig, M. M., Kriegel, H. P., Ng, R. T., & Sander, J. (2000).
//       "LOF: Identifying Density-Based Local Outliers". SIGMOD 2000.
//
//   Mahalanobis:
//     - Mahalanobis, P. C. (1936). "On the generalised distance in
//       statistics". Proceedings of the National Institute of Sciences
//       of India.
//
//   One-Class SVM (упрощённый через RBF):
//     - Schölkopf, B., et al. (2001). "Estimating the support of a
//       high-dimensional distribution". Neural Computation.
//
//   DBSCAN:
//     - Ester, M., Kriegel, H. P., Sander, J., & Xu, X. (1996).
//       "A density-based algorithm for discovering clusters in large
//       spatial databases with noise". KDD 1996.
//
// Версия: 6.0.0

import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// УТИЛИТЫ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function ensureDir(dir) {
  if (!existsSync(dir)) mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
}

function saveJSON(fp, data) {
  ensureDir(dirname(fp));
  writeFileSync(fp, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

function mean(arr) {
  if (arr.length === 0) return 0;
  return arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length;
}

function std(arr, ddof = 1) {
  const n = arr.length;
  if (n <= ddof) return 0;
  const m = mean(arr);
  return Math.sqrt(arr.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - m) ** 2, 0) / (n - ddof));
}

function euclidean(a, b) {
  let s = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += (a[i] - b[i]) ** 2;
  return Math.sqrt(s);
}

function quantile(arr, p) {
  if (arr.length === 0) return 0;
  const sorted = [...arr].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const idx = p * (sorted.length - 1);
  const lo = Math.floor(idx);
  const hi = Math.ceil(idx);
  if (lo === hi) return sorted[lo];
  return sorted[lo] + (sorted[hi] - sorted[lo]) * (idx - lo);
}

/**
 * Нормализация матрицы (z-score по столбцам).
 */
function standardize(X) {
  if (X.length === 0) return { X: [], means: [], stds: [] };
  const n = X.length;
  const d = X[0].length;
  const means = new Array(d).fill(0);
  const stds = new Array(d).fill(0);

  for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) {
    const col = X.map(row => row[j]);
    means[j] = mean(col);
    stds[j] = std(col) || 1;
  }

  const Xs = X.map(row => row.map((v, j) => (v - means[j]) / stds[j]));
  return { X: Xs, means, stds };
}

/**
 * Матричное умножение (n×m · m×p).
 */
function matMul(A, B) {
  const n = A.length;
  const m = A[0].length;
  const p = B[0].length;
  const C = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(p).fill(0));
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < m; k++) {
      const aik = A[i][k];
      if (aik === 0) continue;
      for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) {
        C[i][j] += aik * B[k][j];
      }
    }
  }
  return C;
}

function matTranspose(A) {
  return A[0].map((_, i) => A.map(row => row[i]));
}

/**
 * Обращение матрицы через Gauss-Jordan.
 */
function matInverse(A) {
  const n = A.length;
  const M = A.map((row, i) => [
    ...row,
    ...Array(n).fill(0).map((_, j) => (i === j ? 1 : 0)),
  ]);

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    // Pivot
    let maxRow = i;
    for (let k = i + 1; k < n; k++) {
      if (Math.abs(M[k][i]) > Math.abs(M[maxRow][i])) maxRow = k;
    }
    [M[i], M[maxRow]] = [M[maxRow], M[i]];
    if (Math.abs(M[i][i]) < 1e-12) M[i][i] += 1e-8;

    const pivot = M[i][i];
    for (let j = 0; j < 2 * n; j++) M[i][j] /= pivot;

    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      if (k === i) continue;
      const factor = M[k][i];
      for (let j = 0; j < 2 * n; j++) {
        M[k][j] -= factor * M[i][j];
      }
    }
  }

  return M.map(row => row.slice(n));
}

/**
 * Ковариационная матрица.
 */
function covarianceMatrix(X) {
  const n = X.length;
  const d = X[0].length;
  const means = new Array(d).fill(0);
  for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) {
    means[j] = mean(X.map(row => row[j]));
  }

  const C = Array.from({ length: d }, () => new Array(d).fill(0));
  for (let i = 0; i < d; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) {
      let sum = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
        sum += (X[k][i] - means[i]) * (X[k][j] - means[j]);
      }
      C[i][j] = sum / (n - 1 || 1);
    }
  }

  return { matrix: C, means };
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 1. ISOLATION FOREST
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Узел изоляционного дерева.
 */
class ITreeNode {
  constructor(depth, size) {
    this.depth = depth;
    this.size = size;
    this.feature = null;
    this.threshold = null;
    this.left = null;
    this.right = null;
  }

  get isLeaf() {
    return this.left === null && this.right === null;
  }
}

/**
 * Isolation Tree — рекурсивное разбиение с случайным выбором
 * признака и случайным порогом между min и max.
 */
function buildITree(X, depth, maxDepth) {
  const node = new ITreeNode(depth, X.length);

  if (depth >= maxDepth || X.length <= 1) {
    return node;
  }

  const d = X[0].length;
  const feature = Math.floor(Math.random() * d);
  const values = X.map(row => row[feature]);
  const minV = Math.min(...values);
  const maxV = Math.max(...values);

  if (minV === maxV) {
    return node;
  }

  const threshold = minV + Math.random() * (maxV - minV);
  const left = [];
  const right = [];

  for (const row of X) {
    if (row[feature] < threshold) left.push(row);
    else right.push(row);
  }

  if (left.length === 0 || right.length === 0) {
    return node;
  }

  node.feature = feature;
  node.threshold = threshold;
  node.left = buildITree(left, depth + 1, maxDepth);
  node.right = buildITree(right, depth + 1, maxDepth);

  return node;
}

/**
 * Длина пути точки в дереве (усреднённая до листа).
 */
function pathLength(x, node, depth = 0) {
  if (node.isLeaf) {
    return depth + cFactor(node.size);
  }
  if (x[node.feature] < node.threshold) {
    return pathLength(x, node.left, depth + 1);
  }
  return pathLength(x, node.right, depth + 1);
}

/**
 * Фактор коррекции для незавершённых узлов:
 * c(n) = 2·H(n-1) - 2·(n-1)/n
 * где H(k) ≈ ln(k) + γ (Euler-Mascheroni constant).
 */
function cFactor(n) {
  if (n <= 1) return 0;
  if (n === 2) return 1;
  return 2 * (Math.log(n - 1) + 0.5772156649) - 2 * (n - 1) / n;
}

/**
 * Isolation Forest.
 */
class IsolationForest {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.nTrees = config.nTrees || 100;
    this.sampleSize = config.sampleSize || 256;
    this.contamination = config.contamination ?? 0.05;
    this.trees = [];
    this.threshold = 0;
    this.trained = false;
  }

  /**
   * Обучение.
   */
  fit(X) {
    if (X.length < 2) {
      this.trees = [];
      this.trained = false;
      return this;
    }

    const n = X.length;
    const sampleSize = Math.min(this.sampleSize, n);
    const maxDepth = Math.ceil(Math.log2(sampleSize));
    this.maxDepth = maxDepth;

    this.trees = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < this.nTrees; i++) {
      // Bootstrap sample
      const sample = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < sampleSize; j++) {
        sample.push(X[Math.floor(Math.random() * n)]);
      }
      this.trees.push(buildITree(sample, 0, maxDepth));
    }

    // Вычисление порога по обучающим данным
    const scores = X.map(x => this._score(x));
    this.threshold = quantile(scores, 1 - this.contamination);
    this.trained = true;

    return this;
  }

  /**
   * Anomaly score s(x, n) = 2^(-E[h(x)] / c(n)).
   * > 0.5 — аномалия, < 0.5 — норма.
   */
  _score(x) {
    if (this.trees.length === 0) return 0.5;
    const avgPath = mean(this.trees.map(t => pathLength(x, t)));
    const c = cFactor(this.sampleSize);
    return Math.pow(2, -avgPath / Math.max(c, 1e-10));
  }

  /**
   * Предсказание одной точки.
   */
  predictOne(x) {
    const score = this._score(x);
    return {
      score,
      isAnomaly: score > this.threshold,
      threshold: this.threshold,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Пакетное предсказание.
   */
  predict(X) {
    return X.map(x => this.predictOne(x));
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 2. LOCAL OUTLIER FACTOR (LOF)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * LOF: сравнение локальной плотности точки с плотностью её соседей.
 *
 * k-distance(p) = расстояние до k-го ближайшего соседа
 * N_k(p) = все точки в пределах k-distance
 * reach-dist_k(p, o) = max(k-distance(o), d(p, o))
 * lrd_k(p) = 1 / mean(reach-dist_k(p, o)) для o ∈ N_k(p)
 * LOF_k(p) = mean(lrd_k(o) / lrd_k(p)) для o ∈ N_k(p)
 *
 * LOF ≈ 1 — норма, LOF > 1.5 — аномалия.
 */
class LocalOutlierFactor {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.k = config.k || 20;
    this.X = null;
    this.lofValues = null;
    this.lrd = null;
    this.kDistances = null;
    this.kNeighbors = null;
    this.threshold = 1.5;
  }

  /**
   * Обучение.
   */
  fit(X) {
    this.X = X;
    const n = X.length;
    const k = Math.min(this.k, n - 1);

    if (k < 1) {
      this.lofValues = new Array(n).fill(1);
      return this;
    }

    // 1. k-расстояния и k-соседи
    this.kDistances = new Array(n);
    this.kNeighbors = new Array(n);

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const distances = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
        if (i === j) continue;
        distances.push({ j, d: euclidean(X[i], X[j]) });
      }
      distances.sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d);
      this.kDistances[i] = distances[k - 1].d;
      this.kNeighbors[i] = distances.slice(0, k);
    }

    // 2. Local reachability density (LRD)
    this.lrd = new Array(n);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      let sumReach = 0;
      for (const { j, d } of this.kNeighbors[i]) {
        sumReach += Math.max(this.kDistances[j], d);
      }
      this.lrd[i] = this.kNeighbors[i].length / Math.max(sumReach, 1e-10);
    }

    // 3. LOF values
    this.lofValues = new Array(n);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      let sum = 0;
      for (const { j } of this.kNeighbors[i]) {
        sum += this.lrd[j] / Math.max(this.lrd[i], 1e-10);
      }
      this.lofValues[i] = sum / Math.max(this.kNeighbors[i].length, 1);
    }

    // Threshold — квантиль 1 - contamination (default 0.05)
    this.threshold = quantile(this.lofValues, 0.95);

    return this;
  }

  /**
   * LOF для новой точки.
   */
  scoreOne(x) {
    if (!this.X) return 1;
    const n = this.X.length;
    const k = Math.min(this.k, n);

    const distances = this.X.map((xi, j) => ({ j, d: euclidean(x, xi) }));
    distances.sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d);
    const neighbors = distances.slice(0, k);

    let sumReach = 0;
    for (const { j, d } of neighbors) {
      sumReach += Math.max(this.kDistances[j], d);
    }
    const lrdX = k / Math.max(sumReach, 1e-10);

    let sumRatio = 0;
    for (const { j } of neighbors) {
      sumRatio += this.lrd[j] / Math.max(lrdX, 1e-10);
    }
    return sumRatio / k;
  }

  predictOne(x) {
    const lof = this.scoreOne(x);
    return { lof, isAnomaly: lof > this.threshold, threshold: this.threshold };
  }

  predict(X) {
    return X.map(x => this.predictOne(x));
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 3. MAHALANOBIS DISTANCE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Mahalanobis: расстояние с учётом ковариационной структуры.
 * d(x) = sqrt((x - μ)ᵀ Σ⁻¹ (x - μ))
 */
class MahalanobisDetector {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.contamination = config.contamination ?? 0.05;
    this.meanVec = null;
    this.covMatrix = null;
    this.covInv = null;
    this.threshold = 0;
  }

  fit(X) {
    if (X.length < 3) return this;

    const { matrix, means } = covarianceMatrix(X);

    // Регуляризация: добавляем ε на диагональ для устойчивости
    const d = matrix.length;
    const regularized = matrix.map((row, i) =>
      row.map((v, j) => v + (i === j ? 1e-6 : 0))
    );

    this.meanVec = means;
    this.covMatrix = regularized;

    try {
      this.covInv = matInverse(regularized);
    } catch (e) {
      // Fallback: диагональная ковариация
      this.covInv = regularized.map((row, i) =>
        row.map((_, j) => i === j ? 1 / Math.max(regularized[i][i], 1e-6) : 0)
      );
    }

    // Вычисление порога
    const distances = X.map(x => this._distance(x));
    this.threshold = quantile(distances, 1 - this.contamination);

    return this;
  }

  _distance(x) {
    if (!this.covInv) return 0;
    const d = x.length;
    const diff = x.map((xi, i) => xi - this.meanVec[i]);

    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < d; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < d; j++) {
        sum += diff[i] * this.covInv[i][j] * diff[j];
      }
    }
    return Math.sqrt(Math.max(sum, 0));
  }

  predictOne(x) {
    const distance = this._distance(x);
    return { distance, isAnomaly: distance > this.threshold, threshold: this.threshold };
  }

  predict(X) {
    return X.map(x => this.predictOne(x));
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 4. ONE-CLASS SVM (упрощённый через RBF)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Упрощённый One-Class SVM через RBF-ядро.
 *
 * Идея: строим границу вокруг "нормальных" точек, используя
 * взвешенную сумму гауссовых ядер с центром в опорных точках.
 * Точки далеко от всех центров → аномалии.
 *
 * Здесь используется аппроксимация: k(x) = Σ exp(-||x - x_i||²/(2σ²))
 * Устанавливаем порог на 5-й перцентиль ядерной функции на обучающих данных.
 */
class OneClassSVM {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.gamma = config.gamma ?? 0.1; // 1/(2σ²)
    this.contamination = config.contamination ?? 0.05;
    this.supportVectors = null;
    this.threshold = 0;
  }

  fit(X) {
    // Уменьшаем число support vectors если много точек
    const maxSV = 100;
    this.supportVectors = X.length <= maxSV
      ? X
      : this._subsample(X, maxSV);

    const scores = X.map(x => this._score(x));
    this.threshold = quantile(scores, this.contamination);

    return this;
  }

  _subsample(X, n) {
    const indices = new Set();
    while (indices.size < n) {
      indices.add(Math.floor(Math.random() * X.length));
    }
    return [...indices].map(i => X[i]);
  }

  _score(x) {
    if (!this.supportVectors) return 0;
    let sum = 0;
    for (const sv of this.supportVectors) {
      const dist2 = sv.reduce((s, v, i) => s + (v - x[i]) ** 2, 0);
      sum += Math.exp(-this.gamma * dist2);
    }
    return sum / this.supportVectors.length;
  }

  predictOne(x) {
    const score = this._score(x);
    return { score, isAnomaly: score < this.threshold, threshold: this.threshold };
  }

  predict(X) {
    return X.map(x => this.predictOne(x));
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 5. DBSCAN-BASED OUTLIER DETECTION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * DBSCAN для обнаружения шума.
 * Точки, не попавшие ни в один кластер (label = -1) — аномалии.
 */
class DBSCANOutlier {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.eps = config.eps ?? 1;
    this.minPts = config.minPts ?? 5;
    this.labels = null;
    this.clusters = 0;
  }

  fit(X) {
    const n = X.length;
    const labels = new Array(n).fill(-1); // -1 = шум
    let clusterId = 0;

    const visited = new Array(n).fill(false);

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      if (visited[i]) continue;
      visited[i] = true;

      const neighbors = this._regionQuery(X, i);

      if (neighbors.length < this.minPts) {
        labels[i] = -1; // noise
      } else {
        this._expandCluster(X, labels, i, neighbors, clusterId, visited);
        clusterId++;
      }
    }

    this.labels = labels;
    this.clusters = clusterId;
    return this;
  }

  _regionQuery(X, i) {
    const neighbors = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < X.length; j++) {
      if (euclidean(X[i], X[j]) <= this.eps) neighbors.push(j);
    }
    return neighbors;
  }

  _expandCluster(X, labels, i, neighbors, clusterId, visited) {
    labels[i] = clusterId;
    const queue = [...neighbors];

    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const j = queue.shift();
      if (!visited[j]) {
        visited[j] = true;
        const newNeighbors = this._regionQuery(X, j);
        if (newNeighbors.length >= this.minPts) {
          for (const k of newNeighbors) {
            if (!queue.includes(k) && labels[k] === -1) queue.push(k);
          }
        }
      }
      if (labels[j] === -1) labels[j] = clusterId;
    }
  }

  predictOne() {
    // DBSCAN не поддерживает онлайн-предсказание для новых точек
    return { isAnomaly: false };
  }

  isOutlier(i) {
    return this.labels && this.labels[i] === -1;
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 6. АНСАМБЛЬ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Ансамбль аномалий: голосование нескольких детекторов.
 * Каждая точка получает "голоса" (0..5) — сколько детекторов её отметили.
 */
class AnomalyEnsemble {
  constructor(config = {}) {
    this.contamination = config.contamination ?? 0.05;
    this.isolationForest = new IsolationForest({ contamination: this.contamination });
    this.lof = new LocalOutlierFactor({ k: config.lofK || 20 });
    this.mahalanobis = new MahalanobisDetector({ contamination: this.contamination });
    this.oneClassSVM = new OneClassSVM({ contamination: this.contamination });
    this.dbscan = new DBSCANOutlier({ eps: config.dbscanEps || 1.5, minPts: config.dbscanMinPts || 5 });
    this.trained = false;
  }

  fit(X) {
    this.isolationForest.fit(X);
    this.lof.fit(X);
    this.mahalanobis.fit(X);
    this.oneClassSVM.fit(X);
    this.dbscan.fit(X);
    this.trained = true;
    return this;
  }

  predictOne(x, dbscanIndex = null) {
    const ifResult = this.isolationForest.predictOne(x);
    const lofResult = this.lof.predictOne(x);
    const mahResult = this.mahalanobis.predictOne(x);
    const svmResult = this.oneClassSVM.predictOne(x);
    const dbResult = dbscanIndex !== null
      ? { isAnomaly: this.dbscan.isOutlier(dbscanIndex) }
      : { isAnomaly: false };

    const votes = [
      ifResult.isAnomaly,
      lofResult.isAnomaly,
      mahResult.isAnomaly,
      svmResult.isAnomaly,
      dbResult.isAnomaly,
    ].filter(Boolean).length;

    // Взвешенный score
    const avgScore = (
      ifResult.score * 0.3 +
      Math.min(1, lofResult.lof / 2) * 0.25 +
      Math.min(1, mahResult.distance / 5) * 0.2 +
      (1 - svmResult.score) * 0.15 +
      (dbResult.isAnomaly ? 1 : 0) * 0.1
    );

    return {
      isAnomaly: votes >= 2, // минимум 2 детектора
      votes,
      totalDetectors: 5,
      avgScore,
      details: {
        isolationForest: ifResult,
        lof: lofResult,
        mahalanobis: mahResult,
        oneClassSVM: svmResult,
        dbscan: dbResult,
      },
    };
  }

  predict(X) {
    return X.map((x, i) => this.predictOne(x, i));
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 7. ИНТЕГРАЦИЯ С CRUCIX
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Извлечение признаков из sweep для anomaly detection.
 */
function sweepToFeatureVector(s) {
  return [
    (s.fred?.vix ?? 20) / 50,
    (s.fred?.hySpread ?? 3) / 10,
    (s.fred?.treasury10y ?? 4) / 6,
    (s.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length ?? 0) / 20,
    (s.sanctions?.count ?? 0) / 10,
    (s.energy?.oilPrice ?? 70) / 120,
    (s.gold?.price ?? 1900) / 2200,
    (s.dxy?.value ?? 100) / 110,
    (s.radiation ? Math.max(...Object.values(s.radiation).map(r => r.cpm || 0)) : 0) / 200,
    (s.delta?.newAlerts ?? 0) / 10,
    (s.delta?.escalatedAlerts ?? 0) / 5,
  ];
}

/**
 * Полный anomaly detection цикл для Crucix.
 *
 * @param {Array} history — массив sweep-объектов
 * @param {Object} options — параметры
 * @returns {Object} — агрегированный результат
 */
export function crucixAnomalyDetection(history, options = {}) {
  if (!history || history.length < 20) {
    return {
      module: 'anomaly_detection',
      available: false,
      reason: 'insufficient_history',
      minimumRequired: 20,
      actual: history.length,
    };
  }

  const t0 = Date.now();
  console.log(`[anomaly_detection] Запуск на ${history.length} sweep'ах`);

  const result = {
    module: 'anomaly_detection',
    available: true,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    nSweeps: history.length,
  };

  // Извлечение признаков
  const X = history.map(sweepToFeatureVector);

  // Стандартизация
  const { X: Xs, means, stds } = standardize(X);

  // Ансамбль
  const ensemble = new AnomalyEnsemble({
    contamination: options.contamination ?? 0.05,
    lofK: options.lofK || 20,
    dbscanEps: options.dbscanEps || 1.5,
    dbscanMinPts: options.dbscanMinPts || 5,
  });

  try {
    ensemble.fit(Xs);
  } catch (e) {
    result.error = `ensemble fit failed: ${e.message}`;
    result.elapsedMs = Date.now() - t0;
    return result;
  }

  const predictions = ensemble.predict(Xs);

  // Статистика по детекторам
  const ifAnomalies = predictions.filter(p => p.details.isolationForest.isAnomaly).length;
  const lofAnomalies = predictions.filter(p => p.details.lof.isAnomaly).length;
  const mahAnomalies = predictions.filter(p => p.details.mahalanobis.isAnomaly).length;
  const svmAnomalies = predictions.filter(p => p.details.oneClassSVM.isAnomaly).length;
  const dbAnomalies = predictions.filter(p => p.details.dbscan.isAnomaly).length;

  // Список аномальных sweep'ов
  const anomalies = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < predictions.length; i++) {
    if (predictions[i].isAnomaly) {
      anomalies.push({
        index: i,
        timestamp: history[i].timestamp,
        votes: predictions[i].votes,
        score: Math.round(predictions[i].avgScore * 1000) / 1000,
        features: X[i].map((v, j) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100),
        details: predictions[i].details,
      });
    }
  }

  // Аномальность последнего sweep
  const lastPrediction = predictions[predictions.length - 1];

  // Топ-5 самых аномальных sweep'ов
  const topAnomalies = [...predictions]
    .map((p, i) => ({ index: i, ...p }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.avgScore - a.avgScore)
    .slice(0, 5)
    .map(p => ({
      index: p.index,
      timestamp: history[p.index].timestamp,
      score: Math.round(p.avgScore * 1000) / 1000,
      votes: p.votes,
    }));

  // Объяснение аномалий: какие признаки отклонились
  let anomalyExplanation = null;
  if (lastPrediction.isAnomaly) {
    const lastVec = Xs[Xs.length - 1];
    const featureNames = ['vix', 'hySpread', 'treasury10y', 'conflicts', 'sanctions',
                          'oilPrice', 'goldPrice', 'dxy', 'radiation', 'newAlerts', 'escalatedAlerts'];
    const deviations = lastVec.map((z, j) => ({
      feature: featureNames[j],
      zScore: Math.round(z * 100) / 100,
      rawValue: X[X.length - 1][j],
    })).sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.zScore) - Math.abs(a.zScore));

    anomalyExplanation = {
      topDeviations: deviations.slice(0, 5),
      interpretation: `Последний sweep аномален. Отклонения: ${deviations.slice(0, 3).map(d => `${d.feature} (z=${d.zScore})`).join(', ')}`,
    };
  }

  result.summary = {
    nAnomalies: anomalies.length,
    anomalyRate: Math.round(anomalies.length / history.length * 1000) / 1000,
    detectors: {
      isolationForest: ifAnomalies,
      lof: lofAnomalies,
      mahalanobis: mahAnomalies,
      oneClassSVM: svmAnomalies,
      dbscan: dbAnomalies,
    },
  };

  result.lastSweep = {
    isAnomaly: lastPrediction.isAnomaly,
    votes: lastPrediction.votes,
    score: Math.round(lastPrediction.avgScore * 1000) / 1000,
  };

  result.topAnomalies = topAnomalies;
  result.anomalies = anomalies.slice(-20); // последние 20
  result.explanation = anomalyExplanation;

  result.interpretation = lastPrediction.isAnomaly
    ? `⚠ Последний sweep аномален (${lastPrediction.votes}/5 детекторов согласны)`
    : `Нормальный режим. Всего аномалий: ${anomalies.length} (${(anomalies.length / history.length * 100).toFixed(1)}%)`;

  result.elapsedMs = Date.now() - t0;

  // Сохранение
  const dir = join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'runs', 'predictions');
  ensureDir(dir);
  saveJSON(join(dir, 'anomaly_detection.json'), result);

  console.log(`[anomaly_detection] Цикл завершён за ${result.elapsedMs}ms`);
  return result;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 8. ЭКСПОРТ
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export {
  IsolationForest,
  LocalOutlierFactor,
  MahalanobisDetector,
  OneClassSVM,
  DBSCANOutlier,
  AnomalyEnsemble,
  standardize,
  covarianceMatrix,
};

// models/hawkes.mjs
// Процесс Хоукса (Hawkes process) — самовозбуждающий точечный процесс
// Источник: сейсмология (mainshock -> aftershocks)
// Применение в Crucix: кластеризация конфликтов, рыночных шоков, кибератак
//
// Теоретическая основа:
//   Hawkes, A. G. (1971). "Spectra of some self-exciting and mutually
//   exciting point processes". Biometrika.
//
// λ(t) = μ + Σ θ · exp(-β · (t - t_i))  для t > t_i
// где:
//   μ — фоновая интенсивность
//   θ — сила возбуждения (сколько афтершоков порождает событие)
//   β — скорость затухания возбуждения
//   n = θ/β — branching factor (n ≥ 1 = взрывной режим)

/**
 * Процесс Хоукса с экспоненциальным ядром (самый распространённый)
 */
class HawkesProcess {
  constructor({ mu = 0.5, theta = 0.8, beta = 1.0 } = {}) {
    this.mu = mu;
    this.theta = theta;
    this.beta = beta;
    this.events = [];
    this.history = [];
  }

  /**
   * Условная интенсивность λ(t) в момент t.
   * Сложность O(N) — перебор всех прошлых событий.
   * Для больших N использовать рекурсивную форму:
   *   A(t) = exp(-β·(t - t_last)) · (A(t_last) + 1)
   */
  intensity(t) {
    let lambda = this.mu;
    for (let i = this.events.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const ti = this.events[i];
      if (ti >= t) continue;
      lambda += this.theta * Math.exp(-this.beta * (t - ti));
    }
    return lambda;
  }

  /**
   * Рекурсивная версия интенсивности — O(1) при потоковой обработке.
   * Использует 2 скаляра: A (накопленное возбуждение) и t_last.
   */
  intensityRecursive(t, state) {
    const decay = Math.exp(-this.beta * (t - state.tLast));
    const A = state.A * decay;
    return { lambda: this.mu + A, A };
  }

  /**
   * Добавление наблюдаемого события
   */
  addEvent(t) {
    // Поддерживаем сортировку
    let i = this.events.length;
    while (i > 0 && this.events[i - 1] > t) i--;
    this.events.splice(i, 0, t);
  }

  /**
   * Лог-правдоподобие:
   *   L(μ, θ, β) = Σ log λ(t_i) - ∫₀ᵀ λ(t) dt
   *
   * Компенсатор ∫₀ᵀ λ(t)dt = μT + Σ (θ/β)(1 - exp(-β(T - t_i)))
   */
  logLikelihood() {
    if (this.events.length === 0) return 0;
    const T = this.events[this.events.length - 1];

    let logL = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.events.length; i++) {
      const ti = this.events[i];
      // Интенсивность в момент ti — только события до ti
      let lambda = this.mu;
      for (let j = 0; j < i; j++) {
        lambda += this.theta * Math.exp(-this.beta * (ti - this.events[j]));
      }
      logL += Math.log(Math.max(lambda, 1e-10));
    }

    // Компенсатор
    let integral = this.mu * T;
    for (const ti of this.events) {
      integral += (this.theta / this.beta) * (1 - Math.exp(-this.beta * (T - ti)));
    }
    logL -= integral;

    return logL;
  }

  /**
   * Оценка параметров методом сеточного поиска (grid search).
   * В продакшене заменить на L-BFGS или Adam.
   */
  fit(events, opts = {}) {
    const {
      muRange = [0.01, 0.05, 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0],
      thetaRange = [0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5],
      betaRange = [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 10.0],
    } = opts;

    this.events = [...events].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    let bestParams = { mu: this.mu, theta: this.theta, beta: this.beta };
    let bestLL = -Infinity;

    for (const mu of muRange) {
      for (const theta of thetaRange) {
        for (const beta of betaRange) {
          this.mu = mu;
          this.theta = theta;
          this.beta = beta;
          const ll = this.logLikelihood();
          if (ll > bestLL) {
            bestLL = ll;
            bestParams = { mu, theta, beta };
          }
        }
      }
    }

    this.mu = bestParams.mu;
    this.theta = bestParams.theta;
    this.beta = bestParams.beta;
    return { ...bestParams, logLikelihood: bestLL };
  }

  /**
   * Прогноз: ожидаемое количество событий в [T, T+horizon].
   * E[N(t, t+h)] = μh + Σ θ/β · exp(-β(T-t_i)) · (1 - exp(-βh))
   */
  forecastCount(horizon) {
    if (this.events.length === 0) return this.mu * horizon;

    const T = this.events[this.events.length - 1];
    let expected = this.mu * horizon;

    for (const ti of this.events) {
      const dt = T - ti;
      const residual = this.theta * Math.exp(-this.beta * dt) *
        (1 - Math.exp(-this.beta * horizon)) / this.beta;
      expected += residual;
    }

    return expected;
  }

  /**
   * Вероятность хотя бы одного события в горизонте (Пуассоновское приближение):
   *   P(N > 0) = 1 - exp(-E[N])
   */
  forecastProbability(horizon) {
    const expected = this.forecastCount(horizon);
    return 1 - Math.exp(-expected);
  }

  /**
   * Branching factor — критический параметр.
   *   n = θ/β. Если n < 1 — стационарен, n ≥ 1 — взрывной (эпидемия).
   */
  branchingFactor() {
    return this.theta / this.beta;
  }

  /**
   * Сериализация для сохранения в runs/predictions/
   */
  serialize() {
    return JSON.stringify({
      mu: this.mu,
      theta: this.theta,
      beta: this.beta,
      events: this.events,
      branchingFactor: this.branchingFactor(),
    });
  }

  static deserialize(json) {
    const data = JSON.parse(json);
    const hp = new HawkesProcess(data);
    hp.events = data.events || [];
    return hp;
  }
}

/**
 * Конвертация sweep-данных в события для процесса Хоукса.
 * Каждое конфликтное событие GDELT -> отдельная временная метка.
 * Время в секундах (Unix epoch), с небольшим разбросом внутри sweep.
 */
function sweepToHawkesEvents(history) {
  const events = [];
  for (const sweep of history) {
    const timestamp = new Date(sweep.timestamp).getTime() / 1000;
    const count = sweep.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length || 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(count, 20); i++) {
      events.push(timestamp + i * 0.1);
    }
  }
  return events;
}

export { HawkesProcess, sweepToHawkesEvents };

// apis/predict/exotic/chaos.mjs
// Chaos Theory: Lyapunov, correlation dimension, recurrence plots

function euclidean(a, b) { let s = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += (a[i] - b[i]) ** 2; return Math.sqrt(s); }
function linearRegressionSlope(x, y) { const n = x.length; const mx = x.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n; const my = y.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n; let num = 0, den = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { num += (x[i] - mx) * (y[i] - my); den += (x[i] - mx) ** 2; } return den > 0 ? num / den : 0; }
function takensEmbedding(series, m, tau = 1) { const n = series.length; const embedded = []; for (let i = 0; i < n - (m - 1) * tau; i++) { const point = []; for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) point.push(series[i + j * tau]); embedded.push(point); } return embedded; }
function lyapunovExponent(series, { m = 3, tau = 1, maxIterations = 100 } = {}) { const embedded = takensEmbedding(series, m, tau); const n = embedded.length; if (n < 20) return { error: 'Мало точек', count: n }; const distances = Array.from({ length: maxIterations }, () => []); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { let bestDist = Infinity, bestJ = -1; for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) { if (Math.abs(i - j) < m) continue; const d = euclidean(embedded[i], embedded[j]); if (d < bestDist && d > 0) { bestDist = d; bestJ = j; } } if (bestJ === -1) continue; for (let k = 0; k < maxIterations; k++) { if (i + k >= n || bestJ + k >= n) break; const d = euclidean(embedded[i + k], embedded[bestJ + k]); if (d > 0) distances[k].push(Math.log(d)); } } const avgLog = distances.map(arr => arr.length > 0 ? arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / arr.length : 0); const fitLen = Math.max(3, Math.floor(avgLog.length * 0.2)); const x = Array.from({ length: fitLen }, (_, i) => i); const y = avgLog.slice(0, fitLen); const slope = linearRegressionSlope(x, y); return { lyapunovExponent: slope, regime: slope > 0.05 ? 'chaotic' : slope > 0.005 ? 'weakly chaotic' : 'stable', interpretation: slope > 0.05 ? 'система хаотична' : slope > 0.005 ? 'слабо хаотичная' : 'система устойчива' }; }
function correlationDimension(series, { m = 3, tau = 1, nRadii = 20 } = {}) { const embedded = takensEmbedding(series, m, tau); const n = embedded.length; if (n < 20) return { error: 'Мало точек' }; const distances = []; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) distances.push(euclidean(embedded[i], embedded[j])); if (distances.length === 0) return { error: 'Нет пар' }; const minD = Math.min(...distances); const maxD = Math.max(...distances); const radii = [], correlations = []; for (let i = 0; i < nRadii; i++) { const r = minD * Math.pow(maxD / minD, i / (nRadii - 1)); const count = distances.filter(d => d < r).length; radii.push(r); correlations.push(count / distances.length); } const valid = []; for (let i = 0; i < radii.length; i++) if (correlations[i] > 0.01 && correlations[i] < 0.99) valid.push({ x: Math.log(radii[i]), y: Math.log(correlations[i]) }); if (valid.length < 3) return { error: 'Мало данных' }; const slope = linearRegressionSlope(valid.map(v => v.x), valid.map(v => v.y)); return { dimension: slope, interpretation: slope < 1.5 ? 'низкоразмерная динамика' : slope < 2.5 ? 'умеренная размерность' : 'высокоразмерная (шум)', radii, correlations }; }
function recurrencePlot(series, { m = 3, tau = 1, epsilon = null } = {}) { const embedded = takensEmbedding(series, m, tau); const n = embedded.length; if (n < 10) return { error: 'Мало точек' }; if (epsilon === null) { let maxD = 0; for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(n, 50); i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(n, 50); j++) maxD = Math.max(maxD, euclidean(embedded[i], embedded[j])); epsilon = maxD * 0.1; } const R = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(0)); let recurrenceRate = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) if (euclidean(embedded[i], embedded[j]) < epsilon) { R[i][j] = 1; recurrenceRate++; } recurrenceRate /= (n * n); let diagonalPoints = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n - 2; i++) for (let j = 0; j < n - 2; j++) if (R[i][j] && R[i + 1][j + 1] && R[i + 2][j + 2]) diagonalPoints++; const determinism = diagonalPoints / Math.max(recurrenceRate * n * n, 1); return { recurrenceRate, determinism, epsilon, matrix: R, interpretation: determinism > 0.5 ? 'высокий детерминизм' : determinism > 0.2 ? 'умеренный детерминизм' : 'слабая структура' }; }
function crucixChaos(history) { const vix = history.map(h => h.fred?.vix).filter(v => v !== undefined && !isNaN(v)); if (vix.length < 50) return { error: 'Мало данных' }; const lyap = lyapunovExponent(vix, { m: 3, tau: 1 }); const corr = correlationDimension(vix, { m: 3, tau: 1 }); const recur = recurrencePlot(vix, { m: 3, tau: 1 }); return { lyapunov: lyap, correlationDimension: corr, recurrence: { recurrenceRate: recur.recurrenceRate, determinism: recur.determinism }, predictability: lyap.lyapunovExponent < 0.005 && recur.determinism > 0.3 ? 'high' : lyap.lyapunovExponent < 0.05 ? 'moderate' : 'low' }; }
export { takensEmbedding, lyapunovExponent, correlationDimension, recurrencePlot, crucixChaos, euclidean };

// apis/predict/exotic/game_theory.mjs
// Game Theory для геополитики

function findNashEquilibria(A, B) { const nActions = A.length; const mActions = A[0].length; const equilibria = []; for (let i = 0; i < nActions; i++) { for (let j = 0; j < mActions; j++) { let isBestResponseP1 = true; for (let k = 0; k < nActions; k++) if (A[k][j] > A[i][j]) { isBestResponseP1 = false; break; } let isBestResponseP2 = true; for (let k = 0; k < mActions; k++) if (B[i][k] > B[i][j]) { isBestResponseP2 = false; break; } if (isBestResponseP1 && isBestResponseP2) equilibria.push({ type: 'pure', action1: i, action2: j, payoff1: A[i][j], payoff2: B[i][j] }); } } return equilibria; }
function mixedNash2x2(A, B) { const a11 = A[0][0], a12 = A[0][1], a21 = A[1][0], a22 = A[1][1]; const b11 = B[0][0], b12 = B[0][1], b21 = B[1][0], b22 = B[1][1]; const denomQ = (a11 - a12 - a21 + a22); const q = denomQ !== 0 ? (a22 - a12) / denomQ : 0.5; const denomP = (b11 - b21 - b12 + b22); const p = denomP !== 0 ? (b22 - b21) / denomP : 0.5; if (p >= 0 && p <= 1 && q >= 0 && q <= 1) return { type: 'mixed', p, q, payoff1: p * (q * a11 + (1 - q) * a12) + (1 - p) * (q * a21 + (1 - q) * a22), payoff2: q * (p * b11 + (1 - p) * b21) + (1 - q) * (p * b12 + (1 - p) * b22) }; return null; }
function evolutionarilyStableStrategy(A) { const a11 = A[0][0], a12 = A[0][1], a21 = A[1][0], a22 = A[1][1]; const strategies = []; if (a11 > a21) strategies.push({ type: 'pure', strategy: 0, condition: 'A11 > A21' }); if (a22 > a12) strategies.push({ type: 'pure', strategy: 1, condition: 'A22 > A12' }); const p = (a22 - a12) / (a11 - a12 - a21 + a22); if (p > 0 && p < 1) strategies.push({ type: 'mixed', probability: p, condition: 'interior ESS' }); return strategies; }
function prisonersDilemmaTournament(strategies, rounds = 200, iterations = 100) { const T = 5, R = 3, P = 1, S = 0; const scores = {}; for (const s of strategies) scores[s.name] = 0; for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) { for (let i = 0; i < strategies.length; i++) { for (let j = i; j < strategies.length; j++) { const s1 = strategies[i], s2 = strategies[j]; const history1 = [], history2 = []; let score1 = 0, score2 = 0; for (let r = 0; r < rounds; r++) { const move1 = s1.play(history1, history2); const move2 = s2.play(history2, history1); if (move1 && move2) { score1 += R; score2 += R; } else if (move1 && !move2) { score1 += S; score2 += T; } else if (!move1 && move2) { score1 += T; score2 += S; } else { score1 += P; score2 += P; } history1.push(move1); history2.push(move2); } if (i !== j) { scores[s1.name] += score1 / iterations; scores[s2.name] += score2 / iterations; } else scores[s1.name] += score1 / iterations; } } } return Object.entries(scores).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([name, score]) => ({ name, score })); }
function crucixGameTheory(latest) { const A = [[3, -1], [5, 0]]; const B = [[3, 5], [-1, 0]]; const pureEq = findNashEquilibria(A, B); const mixedEq = mixedNash2x2(A, B); const vix = latest.fred?.vix || 20; const conflicts = latest.gdelt?.conflictEvents?.length || 0; const sanctions = latest.sanctions?.recentCount || 0; const usConfrontProb = Math.min(1, (sanctions + Math.max(0, vix - 20) / 5) / 10); const chinaConfrontProb = Math.min(1, (conflicts + sanctions) / 15); return { payoffMatrix: { A, B }, pureEquilibria: pureEq, mixedEquilibrium: mixedEq, currentState: { usConfrontProbability: usConfrontProb, chinaConfrontProbability: chinaConfrontProb, likelyOutcome: usConfrontProb > 0.5 && chinaConfrontProb > 0.5 ? 'mutual_confront' : usConfrontProb > 0.5 ? 'us_advantage' : chinaConfrontProb > 0.5 ? 'china_advantage' : 'cooperation' }, recommendation: mixedEq ? `Стабильное равновесие: США p=${mixedEq.p.toFixed(2)}, Китай q=${mixedEq.q.toFixed(2)}` : 'Равновесие не найдено', interpretation: usConfrontProb > 0.5 && chinaConfrontProb > 0.5 ? 'высокий риск взаимной эскалации' : usConfrontProb > 0.5 || chinaConfrontProb > 0.5 ? 'асимметричная напряжённость' : 'стабильная кооперация' }; }
export { findNashEquilibria, mixedNash2x2, evolutionarilyStableStrategy, prisonersDilemmaTournament, crucixGameTheory };

// Information Geometry: KL, Fisher, Renyi divergences

function klDivergence(P, Q) { if (P.length !== Q.length) throw new Error('Размеры не совпадают'); let kl = 0; for (let i = 0; i < P.length; i++) if (P[i] > 0) kl += P[i] * Math.log(P[i] / Math.max(Q[i], 1e-15)); return kl; }
function jsDivergence(P, Q) { const M = P.map((p, i) => 0.5 * (p + Q[i])); return 0.5 * klDivergence(P, M) + 0.5 * klDivergence(Q, M); }
function renyiDivergence(P, Q, alpha = 2) { if (alpha === 1) return klDivergence(P, Q); let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < P.length; i++) sum += Math.pow(P[i], alpha) * Math.pow(Math.max(Q[i], 1e-15), 1 - alpha); return Math.log(sum) / (alpha - 1); }
function hellingerDistance(P, Q) { let sum = 0; for (let i = 0; i < P.length; i++) sum += (Math.sqrt(P[i]) - Math.sqrt(Q[i])) ** 2; return Math.sqrt(0.5 * sum); }
function fisherNormal(sigma) { return { mu: 1 / (sigma * sigma), sigma: 2 / (sigma * sigma) }; }
function cramerRaoBound(fisherInfo) { return 1 / fisherInfo; }
function mutualInformation(jointCounts, n) { const margX = {}, margY = {}; for (const [key, count] of jointCounts) { const [i, j] = key.split(',').map(Number); margX[i] = (margX[i] || 0) + count; margY[j] = (margY[j] || 0) + count; } let mi = 0; for (const [key, count] of jointCounts) { const [i, j] = key.split(',').map(Number); const pxy = count / n; const px = margX[i] / n; const py = margY[j] / n; mi += pxy * Math.log(pxy / (px * py)); } return mi; }
function entropy(P) { let h = 0; for (const p of P) if (p > 0) h -= p * Math.log2(p); return h; }
function conditionalEntropy(jointCounts, n) { const margY = {}; for (const [key, count] of jointCounts) { const [, j] = key.split(',').map(Number); margY[j] = (margY[j] || 0) + count; } let h = 0; for (const [key, count] of jointCounts) { const [, j] = key.split(',').map(Number); const pxy = count / n; const py = margY[j] / n; h -= pxy * Math.log2(pxy / py); } return h; }
function histogram(arr, bins) { const min = Math.min(...arr); const max = Math.max(...arr); const width = (max - min) / bins || 1; const counts = new Array(bins).fill(0); for (const v of arr) { const idx = Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor((v - min) / width)); counts[idx]++; } return { edges: Array.from({ length: bins + 1 }, (_, i) => min + i * width), counts }; }
function crucixInfoGeo(history) { const vix = history.map(h => h.fred?.vix).filter(v => v !== undefined && !isNaN(v)); if (vix.length < 40) return { error: 'Мало данных' }; const bins = 15; const half = Math.floor(vix.length / 2); const minV = Math.min(...vix); const maxV = Math.max(...vix); const width = (maxV - minV) / bins; const P = new Array(bins).fill(0); const Q = new Array(bins).fill(0); for (const v of vix.slice(0, half)) P[Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor((v - minV) / width))]++; for (const v of vix.slice(half)) Q[Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor((v - minV) / width))]++; const sumP = P.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); const sumQ = Q.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); const Pnorm = P.map(p => (p + 1e-10) / (sumP + bins * 1e-10)); const Qnorm = Q.map(q => (q + 1e-10) / (sumQ + bins * 1e-10)); return { kl: klDivergence(Pnorm, Qnorm), klReverse: klDivergence(Qnorm, Pnorm), js: jsDivergence(Pnorm, Qnorm), hellinger: hellingerDistance(Pnorm, Qnorm), renyi2: renyiDivergence(Pnorm, Qnorm, 2), entropyOlder: entropy(Pnorm), entropyRecent: entropy(Qnorm), distributionShift: jsDivergence(Pnorm, Qnorm) > 0.1 ? 'significant' : jsDivergence(Pnorm, Qnorm) > 0.03 ? 'moderate' : 'minimal' }; }

// apis/predict/exotic/networks.mjs
// Complex Networks: centrality, communities, small-world

function degrees(network) { const result = {}; for (const node of network.nodes) result[node.id] = 0; for (const edge of network.edges) { if (result[edge.from] !== undefined) result[edge.from] += edge.weight || 1; if (result[edge.to] !== undefined) result[edge.to] += edge.weight || 1; } return result; }
function betweennessCentrality(network) { const nodes = network.nodes.map(n => n.id); const CB = {}; for (const n of nodes) CB[n] = 0; const adj = {}; for (const n of nodes) adj[n] = []; for (const edge of network.edges) { adj[edge.from].push(edge.to); adj[edge.to].push(edge.from); } for (const s of nodes) { const stack = []; const pred = {}; const sigma = {}; const dist = {}; const delta = {}; for (const v of nodes) { pred[v] = []; sigma[v] = 0; dist[v] = -1; delta[v] = 0; } sigma[s] = 1; dist[s] = 0; const queue = [s]; while (queue.length > 0) { const v = queue.shift(); stack.push(v); for (const w of adj[v]) { if (dist[w] < 0) { dist[w] = dist[v] + 1; queue.push(w); } if (dist[w] === dist[v] + 1) { sigma[w] += sigma[v]; pred[w].push(v); } } } while (stack.length > 0) { const w = stack.pop(); for (const v of pred[w]) delta[v] += (sigma[v] / sigma[w]) * (1 + delta[w]); if (w !== s) CB[w] += delta[w]; } } const n = nodes.length; const norm = (n - 1) * (n - 2); for (const v of nodes) CB[v] /= norm || 1; return CB; }
function eigenvectorCentrality(network, iterations = 100) { const nodes = network.nodes.map(n => n.id); const A = {}; for (const id of nodes) A[id] = {}; for (const edge of network.edges) { A[edge.from] = A[edge.from] || {}; A[edge.from][edge.to] = (A[edge.from][edge.to] || 0) + (edge.weight || 1); A[edge.to] = A[edge.to] || {}; A[edge.to][edge.from] = (A[edge.to][edge.from] || 0) + (edge.weight || 1); } let x = {}; for (const id of nodes) x[id] = 1; for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) { const xNew = {}; for (const id of nodes) { xNew[id] = 0; for (const neighbor in A[id]) xNew[id] += A[id][neighbor] * x[neighbor]; } const norm = Math.sqrt(Object.values(xNew).reduce((s, v) => s + v * v, 0)); for (const id of nodes) xNew[id] /= norm || 1; x = xNew; } return x; }
function pageRank(network, damping = 0.85, iterations = 100) { const nodes = network.nodes.map(n => n.id); const n = nodes.length; const outLinks = {}; for (const id of nodes) outLinks[id] = []; for (const edge of network.edges) outLinks[edge.from].push(edge.to); let rank = {}; for (const id of nodes) rank[id] = 1 / n; for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) { const newRank = {}; for (const id of nodes) newRank[id] = (1 - damping) / n; for (const id of nodes) { const out = outLinks[id]; if (out.length === 0) continue; const share = damping * rank[id] / out.length; for (const target of out) if (newRank[target] !== undefined) newRank[target] += share; } rank = newRank; } return rank; }
function detectCommunities(network) { const nodes = network.nodes.map(n => n.id); const adj = {}; for (const id of nodes) adj[id] = new Map(); for (const edge of network.edges) { const w = edge.weight || 1; adj[edge.from].set(edge.to, (adj[edge.from].get(edge.to) || 0) + w); adj[edge.to].set(edge.from, (adj[edge.to].get(edge.from) || 0) + w); } const community = {}; for (const id of nodes) community[id] = id; for (let iter = 0; iter < 20; iter++) { let improved = false; for (const node of nodes) { const currentComm = community[node]; const neighborComms = new Map(); for (const [neighbor] of adj[node]) { const c = community[neighbor]; neighborComms.set(c, (neighborComms.get(c) || 0) + 1); } let bestComm = currentComm; let bestGain = 0; for (const [c, count] of neighborComms) { if (c === currentComm) continue; if (count > bestGain) { bestGain = count; bestComm = c; } } if (bestComm !== currentComm) { community[node] = bestComm; improved = true; } } if (!improved) break; } const communities = {}; for (const id of nodes) { if (!communities[community[id]]) communities[community[id]] = []; communities[community[id]].push(id); } const Q = computeModularity(network, community); return { assignment: community, communities: Object.values(communities).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length), modularity: Q, nCommunities: Object.keys(communities).length }; }
function computeModularity(network, community) { const degrees = {}; for (const node of network.nodes) degrees[node.id] = 0; for (const edge of network.edges) { degrees[edge.from] = (degrees[edge.from] || 0) + (edge.weight || 1); degrees[edge.to] = (degrees[edge.to] || 0) + (edge.weight || 1); } const totalWeight = Object.values(degrees).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 2; let Q = 0; for (const edge of network.edges) if (community[edge.from] === community[edge.to]) { const expected = (degrees[edge.from] * degrees[edge.to]) / (2 * totalWeight); Q += (edge.weight || 1) - expected; } return Q / (2 * totalWeight); }
function crucixNetworks(latest) { const network = { nodes: [{ id: 'usa' }, { id: 'china' }, { id: 'russia' }, { id: 'eu' }, { id: 'uk' }, { id: 'japan' }, { id: 'india' }, { id: 'brazil' }, { id: 'saudi' }, { id: 'iran' }, { id: 'turkey' }, { id: 'israel' }, { id: 'ukraine' }, { id: 'taiwan' }, { id: 'n_korea' }, { id: 's_korea' }], edges: [{ from: 'usa', to: 'eu', weight: 1 }, { from: 'usa', to: 'uk', weight: 1 }, { from: 'usa', to: 'japan', weight: 0.9 }, { from: 'usa', to: 's_korea', weight: 0.9 }, { from: 'china', to: 'russia', weight: 0.8 }, { from: 'china', to: 'iran', weight: 0.6 }, { from: 'china', to: 'n_korea', weight: 0.5 }, { from: 'russia', to: 'iran', weight: 0.6 }, { from: 'usa', to: 'china', weight: -1.2 }, { from: 'usa', to: 'russia', weight: -1.3 }, { from: 'china', to: 'taiwan', weight: -1.5 }, { from: 'russia', to: 'ukraine', weight: -1.5 }, { from: 'israel', to: 'iran', weight: -1.4 }] }; const posNetwork = { nodes: network.nodes, edges: network.edges.filter(e => e.weight > 0) }; const deg = degrees(posNetwork); const between = betweennessCentrality(posNetwork); const eigen = eigenvectorCentrality(posNetwork); const pagerank = pageRank(posNetwork); const communities = detectCommunities(posNetwork); const topBetween = Object.entries(between).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 5); return { degrees: deg, betweenness: between, eigenvector: eigen, pageRank: pagerank, communities: communities.communities, modularity: communities.modularity, topByBetweenness: topBetween, bridgeNode: topBetween[0]?.[0] || 'unknown', interpretation: `Мост: ${topBetween[0]?.[0]}. Сообществ: ${communities.nCommunities}, модулярность: ${communities.modularity.toFixed(3)}` }; }
export { degrees, betweennessCentrality, eigenvectorCentrality, pageRank, detectCommunities, crucixNetworks };

// apis/predict/exotic/quantum_sa.mjs
// Quantum-Inspired Simulated Annealing

function quantumAnnealing({ energy, propose, initial, gamma0 = 5.0, gammaMin = 0.01, T0 = 1.0, coolingRate = 0.995, iterations = 10000, tunnelProbability = 0.1, verbose = false }) { let x = [...initial]; let E = energy(x); let gamma = gamma0; let T = T0; let bestX = [...x]; let bestE = E; const history = []; for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) { const xClassical = propose(x); const EClassical = energy(xClassical); const dE = EClassical - E; let accepted = false; if (dE < 0) { x = xClassical; E = EClassical; accepted = true; } else { const pTunnel = tunnelProbability * Math.exp(-gamma / (dE + 0.01)); const pClassical = Math.exp(-dE / T); const pAccept = Math.max(pClassical, pTunnel); if (Math.random() < pAccept) { x = xClassical; E = EClassical; accepted = true; } } if (E < bestE) { bestE = E; bestX = [...x]; } gamma = Math.max(gammaMin, gamma * coolingRate); T = Math.max(1e-8, T * coolingRate); if (iter % 100 === 0) history.push({ iteration: iter, energy: E, bestEnergy: bestE, gamma, temperature: T, accepted }); if (verbose && iter % 500 === 0) console.log(`Iter ${iter}: E=${E.toFixed(6)}, best=${bestE.toFixed(6)}, γ=${gamma.toFixed(4)}, T=${T.toFixed(4)}`); } return { bestX, bestEnergy: bestE, finalEnergy: E, finalGamma: gamma, finalTemperature: T, history, nIterations: iterations }; }
function pathIntegralQMC({ energy, propose, initial, nTrotter = 8, beta = 10, gamma = 1.0, iterations = 5000 }) { const P = nTrotter; const replicas = Array.from({ length: P }, () => [...initial]); const sliceEnergy = (slice) => energy(slice); const totalEnergy = () => { let E = 0; for (let p = 0; p < P; p++) E += sliceEnergy(replicas[p]); const J = -0.5 * Math.log(Math.tanh(beta * gamma / P)); for (let p = 0; p < P; p++) { const next = (p + 1) % P; for (let i = 0; i < initial.length; i++) E += J * replicas[p][i] * replicas[next][i]; } return E; }; let E = totalEnergy(); let bestX = [...replicas[0]]; let bestE = sliceEnergy(replicas[0]); const history = []; for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) { const p = Math.floor(Math.random() * P); const proposed = propose(replicas[p]); const oldSliceE = sliceEnergy(replicas[p]); const newSliceE = sliceEnergy(proposed); const prev = (p - 1 + P) % P; const next = (p + 1) % P; const J = -0.5 * Math.log(Math.tanh(beta * gamma / P)); let couplingOld = 0, couplingNew = 0; for (let i = 0; i < initial.length; i++) { couplingOld += J * replicas[p][i] * (replicas[prev][i] + replicas[next][i]); couplingNew += J * proposed[i] * (replicas[prev][i] + replicas[next][i]); } const dE = (newSliceE + couplingNew) - (oldSliceE + couplingOld); if (dE <= 0 || Math.random() < Math.exp(-dE * beta / P)) { replicas[p] = proposed; E += dE; } const currentSliceE = sliceEnergy(replicas[0]); if (currentSliceE < bestE) { bestE = currentSliceE; bestX = [...replicas[0]]; } if (iter % 100 === 0) history.push({ iteration: iter, energy: E, bestEnergy: bestE }); } return { bestX, bestEnergy: bestE, replicas, history, nTrotter }; }
function quantumHyperparameterSearch({ modelFactory, X, y, paramBounds, iterations = 200 }) { const energy = (params) => { const paramObj = {}; for (let i = 0; i < paramBounds.length; i++) paramObj[paramBounds[i].name] = params[i]; try { const model = modelFactory(paramObj); const n = X.length; const split = Math.floor(n * 0.8); const XTrain = X.slice(0, split); const yTrain = y.slice(0, split); const XVal = X.slice(split); const yVal = y.slice(split); model.fit(XTrain, yTrain); const preds = model.predict(XVal); let mse = 0; for (let i = 0; i < preds.length; i++) { const p = Array.isArray(preds[i]) ? preds[i][0] : preds[i]; mse += (p - yVal[i]) ** 2; } mse /= preds.length; return mse; } catch (e) { return 1e6; } }; const propose = (x) => { const newX = [...x]; const nChanges = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2); for (let c = 0; c < nChanges; c++) { const i = Math.floor(Math.random() * paramBounds.length); const b = paramBounds[i]; newX[i] = b.min + Math.random() * (b.max - b.min); } return newX; }; const initial = paramBounds.map(b => b.min + Math.random() * (b.max - b.min)); const result = quantumAnnealing({ energy, propose, initial, gamma0: 5.0, T0: 1.0, iterations, coolingRate: 0.997 }); const bestParams = {}; for (let i = 0; i < paramBounds.length; i++) bestParams[paramBounds[i].name] = result.bestX[i]; return { bestParams, bestScore: result.bestEnergy, history: result.history, nIterations: iterations }; }
function crucixQuantumSearch(history) { return { note: 'Quantum annealing для hyperparameter search готов к использованию', reference: 'Kadowaki & Nishimori (1998), Finnila et al. (1994)', usage: 'quantumHyperparameterSearch({ modelFactory, X, y, paramBounds })' }; }
export { quantumAnnealing, pathIntegralQMC, quantumHyperparameterSearch, crucixQuantumSearch };
